[\[1b.1\]{D1874}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_shrI gu h+ya sa mA dza sA d+ha naM sid+d+hi saM b+ha ba ni d+hi nA ma/_bod skad du/_dpal gsang ba 'dus pa'i sgrub pa'i thabs dngos grub 'byung ba'i gter zhes bya ba/_
[\[1b.2\]]dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag 'tshal lo/_/zab gsal gnyis med dag pa'i bdag nyid kyis/_/bdag dang gzhan don phun sum tshogs pa'i sku/_/dpal ldan chos kyi dbyings kyi phyag rgya 
[\[1b.3\]]la/_/rgyal sras rgyu dang bcas pa rab 'byung zhing /_/rtag sogs rgyu 'dras khams kun rab bkang nas/_/ji srid bar du don mdzad rgyun mi 'chad/_/khyab bdag rdo rje sems dpa' de dang ni/_/de yi 
[\[1b.4\]]sras rnams la yang phyag 'tshal lo/_/khams gsum rgyu dang mi rgyu kun khyab pa/_/snying ga'i dbus bsam de la brtan sogs skye/_/chos bdag khros pas kyang ni mi gnon pa/_/mi shigs thig le 
[\[1b.5\]]gang yin rtogs par mos/_/blo dang ldan pa rnams kyis pad+ma kha 'byed pa/_/rmongs pa sreg byed snying gi mun pa sel mdzad pa/_/yon tan dag pa'i mkha' la rgyu zhing 'jigs bral mdzad/_/bla 
[\[2a\]]
[\[2a.1\]]ma dam pa rnams kyi zhal snga rgyal gyur cig_/dpal ldan gsang 'dus mtsho chen las/_/bla ma'i brgyud rim gru gzings kyis/_/yid bzhin nor bu rnam pa gsum/_/sman pa'i zhabs kyis rnyed pa'o/_/
[\[2a.2\]]de rnyed dpe mkhyud spangs byas te/_/'di ni thun mong cig shos kyi/_/dngos grub 'byung phyir gter 'dra ba/_/phan phyir bdag gis brtsam par bya/_/gzhung gzhan spangs te bla ma'i brgyud rim gyis/_/dpal dang ldan pa'i 
[\[2a.3\]]mnyam 'dus sgrub pa'i thabs/_/dam pas gsol ba btab phyir rab brod cing /_/dad pas ci nus bdag gis bshad par bya/_/de la kha cig ni yang dag pa'i man ngag dang bral ba rnams ni dam pa ma yin par mkhas 
[\[2a.4\]]par grags pa rnams la bla ma nyid du khas blangs nas de'i lam la zhugs te/_dka' thub dang smyung ba dang /_brtul zhugs la sogs pas don dam pa gcig po la dpyod cing /_'di snyam du bdag gi rgyu'i 'khrul 'khor 
[\[2a.5\]]la sogs pa dang lhan cig tu skyes kyang /_de rnams kyis stong pa'i dngos po ni yod de/_de tsam zhig brtags pas mngon du byed kyang /_dngos po la don dam par zhen pas stong pa nyid mi shes te grol ba dang rgyang ring du 
[\[2b\]]
[\[2b.1\]]'gyur ro/_/ji skad du/_stong pa nyid ni ma shes pas/_/kun la 'das pa des mi shes/_/dngos dang dngos med rnam 'jigs pas/_/stong pa nyid ni shes par bya/_/zhes 'byung bas so/_/yang kha cig dge ba'i bshes gnyen dang phrad zin kyang /_dam pa ma yin pas de'i lam 
[\[2b.2\]]la zhugs te/_bdag la sogs pa dang bral ba'i phung po dang skye mched dang /_khams rnams mi rtag pa la sogs par 'jig cing /_sdom pa'i snod la brten te/_zhugs pa la sogs pa'i lam gyi phung po la sogs pa la grol nas lhag ma dang bcas pa dang /_lhag ma med pa'i mya ngan 
[\[2b.3\]]las 'das pa mngon du byed do/_/'di yang ma rig pa dang 'brel pa'i phyir 'bras bu phyir ldog pa can zhes bya ste/_de yang ji skad du/_rtsa ba'i rnam shes log gyur nas/_/lhag bcas lhag ma med gyur pa'i/_/rnam grol 'bras bu thob 'gyur yang /_/mngon du rgyu ba med pa yi/_/ma rig 
[\[2b.4\]]bag chags dang 'brel phyir/_/rnam par snang mdzad gang chen mtshos/_/de la thugs rjes gzung ba'i phyir/_/dpal ldan go cha'i 'od zer dang /_/phyag gis dbu la reg mdzad nas/_/de yi ro myong zlog mdzad de/_/yang dag lam la 'dzud par mdzad/_/ces gsang ba'i rim pa phye 
[\[2b.5\]]ba las gsungs so/_/yang kha cig ni dge ba'i bshes gnyen dam pa dang phrad du zin kyang /_de rnams kyis brgyud rim mkhyud pas/_sbyin pa la sogs pa pha rol tu phyin pa bcu bskal pa mi nyung bar bsags nas/_sa'i de bzhin nyid du mngon du byed cing rim gyis sa bcu mngon du mthar thug 
[\[2b.6\]]par byed do/_/de'i sprul pa'i sbyor bas dga' ldan gyi gnas nyid du skye ba len pa dang /_gling la sogs pa la gzigs par mdzad nas yum gyi lhums su 'jug pa dang /_lhums nas bltam pa dang /_gzhon nu rol pa mdzad pa dang /_khab bzhes pa dang /_btsun mo'i 'khor gyi nang nas byung 
[\[2b.7\]]ba dang /_rab tu byung ba dang /_dka' ba spyad pa ston pa dang /_de'i tshe bde bar gshegs pa rnams kyis sngon rang nyid mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa'i man ngag rang rig chos kyi sku mngon du byed pa bstan pa'i phyir/_til gyi gong bu bzhin du ched du longs spyod rdzogs pa'i skur bstan te/
[\[3a\]]
[\[3a.1\]]ye shes kyi phyag rgya gnang nas/_snyoms par 'jug pa'i cho gas rtsa ba'i ye shes dmigs pa dang bral ba/_sbyor ba gnyis kyi bdag nyid sngon dang phyis kyi sbyor ba mngon du byas te/_bdud btul chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba la sogs pa mdzad do/_/don 
[\[3a.2\]]de lta bu'i snod dkon par gzigs nas/_sba bar mdzad de sangs rgyas pa'i tshul so sor mthong bar mdzad do/_/bla ma chen po'i zhal nas ji skad du/_ci'i phyir shAkya thub pa yis/_/grangs med bar du tshogs bsags kyang /_/'di don ma rtogs nai rany+dzar/_/ci yang ma yin 
[\[3a.3\]]ting 'dzin gnas/_/de tshe bde gshegs kun gyis kyang /_/thal byung blo chags de bzlog ste/_/nam mkha'i dbyings ltar rnam dag pa'i/_/zab gsal gnyis med rab bstan te/_/nam phyed dus su rgyal ba ltar/_/de nyid bsgoms pas tho rangs su/_/skad cig gis ni yang 
[\[3a.4\]]dag rtogs/_/byang chub snying por zhugs byas nas/_/dpung chen bdud rnams rab bcom ste/_/sems can gzung bar bya ba'i phyir/_/chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba'o/_/de nas bzung ste don mchog 'di/_/skal ba ldan pa 'ga' zhig la/_/zhal nas zhal dang snyan nas snyan/_/
[\[3a.5\]]brgyud de nges pa'i don bstan pa'o/_/zhes gsungs so/_/de bas na sangs rgyas thams cad sangs rgyas pa'i tshul 'dis shes par bya'o/_/gal te lam gzhan gyis sangs rgyas pa srid na ni/_de'i bla ma dang bcas par 'gyur ro/_/de bas na 'bras bu sku gsum ni gcig kho na ste 
[\[3a.6\]]de'i lam ni sngags kyi spyod pa 'di'o/_/ji skad du rnam par snang mdzad byang chub pa'i rgyud las/_sangs rgyas dus gsum las byung ba/_/sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje can/_/gzhal med ye shes thob pa ni/_/rdo rje sngags kyi don sgom yin/_/zhes 'byung bas so/_/sngags kyi spyod pa 
[\[3a.7\]]la mos pa de dag kyang bzhir shes par bya'o/_/kha cig ni phyi'i gtsang sbra la sogs pa dang /_bdag gi de kho na nyid la sogs pas lhag pa'i lha mnyes par byas pas mngon par mtho ba'i dpal nyams su myong bar byas nas/_bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs rdzogs par byas 
[\[3b\]]
[\[3b.1\]]nas sangs rgyas kyi go 'phang thob par 'dod do/_/yang kha cig ni phyi'i gtsang sbra la sogs pa bya ba dang bya ba ma yin pa spangs nas/_mtshan ma dang bcas pa dang /_mtshan ma med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis mngon par mtho ba'i dpal nyams su myong nas/_tshogs gnyis bsags 
[\[3b.2\]]nas sangs rgyas kyi go 'phang thob par 'dod do/_/yang kha cig ni gtsang sbra la sogs pa smad cing nang gi rnal 'byor dam tshig sems dpa' dang /_phyi rol gyi ye shes sems dpa' phyag rgya bzhi dang ldan pa mnyes par bya ba la sogs pa'i rim pas rang gi lha mnyes par byas pas 
[\[3b.3\]]mngon par mtho ba'i dpal nyams su myong nas/_bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs rdzogs par byas nas sangs rgyas kyi go 'phang thob par 'dod do/_/de dag kyang bcom ldan 'das kyis de lta bu la mos pa'i gdul bya gzung ba'i phyir thabs gsungs pa ste/_phyag rgya chen po'i lhan cig byed pa 
[\[3b.4\]]yin par shes par bya'o/_/phyag rgya chen po'i lhan cig byed pa ni 'di lta ste/_gang dag sems ji lta ba bzhin du brtag pa'i thabs la/_brtson 'grus 'bar ba dang ldan pa rnams kyis bskyed pa dang rdzogs pa'i rim pas skad cig re re la yang snod dang bcud dang lam dang 'bras 
[\[3b.5\]]bur bcas pa sbyong bar byed cing /_rnal 'byor dang phyag rgya la brten nas tshe 'di nyid las rab dang rab kyi mthar phyin par 'gyur ba la som nyi med pas bla na med pa'o/_/de ston par byed pa'i gzhung ni dpal ldan 'dus pa ste/_ji lta bu zhe na/_chos phung brgyad khri bzhi stong dag_/'dus phyir 
[\[3b.6\]]snod yang dam pa'o/_/rmongs pa'i mun nag [(]sal [,]sel [)]bas na/_/sgron ma'i mchog ni 'di yin no/_/rta rgod lta bu'i sems 'dul bas/_/'thad pa'i srab skyogs 'di yin no/_/rnam shes glang chen yan lta bu/_/'gugs phyir lcags kyu dam pa'o/_/rnal 'byor mchog tu nges 
[\[3b.7\]]pa yi/_/dam sdom can gyi tshong 'dus so/_/'khor ba'i rgya mtsho chen po las/_/sgrol byed gru ni chen po'o/_/mngon mtho nges legs khang bzang gi_/steng du phyin byed them skas so/_/rnam rtog las byung nyon mongs kyi/_/grong khyer 'joms shing grol ba yi/_/'khor lo 
[\[4a\]]
[\[4a.1\]]skyong phyir rgyal po'o/_/bcom ldan rdo rje 'chang chen pos/_/nye bar bstan pa'i mdo sde dang /_/de 'dra'i tshul len rgyud 'di ni/_/kun gyi phyi ma gsungs pa ni/_/don rnams de ru gtan la phab/_/'di don sgom byed rnal 'byor pa/_/ma 'ongs rdo rje 'chang 
[\[4a.2\]]ba bas/_/dga' dang rtsod pa med pa dang /_/srung ba dang ni dran pa dang /_/bag yod pa yis brtson bya'o/_/de lta bu'i gzhung de la rtog pa mngon du gtong ba'i nyan pa po rnams khas len pa'i yan lag dang /_gleng gzhi phun sum tshogs pa'i rnam par bshad pas/_gang zag 
[\[4a.3\]]gzhan yid brtan du rung bar bya ba ni bsdus pa'i don du bshad pas der zhes bya'o/_/ye shes las sprul pa'i 'khor de bzhin gshegs pa la sogs pa rnams la rgyud kyi don ji ltar bstan pa yang der shes par bya ste/_de'i phyis 'byung ba'i don rje btsun rnams kyis snod du rung bar 
[\[4a.4\]]byas pa'i gang zag la rgyud kyi don ji ltar bsdus te bstan pa de sgrub thabs kyi rim pa 'dir rgyas par bshad par bya'o/_/de la dang po snying rje'i stobs las skyes/_/byang chub sems ldan sbyin pa la sogs brgyan/_/dpal ldan 'dus pa'i 'khor lor cho ga bzhin/_/dbang 
[\[4a.5\]]bskur gnang ba thob pas rim 'di bya/_/gang zhig dgon pa chen po'i sa phyogs sogs/_/gang rung gnas su stegs bu gru bzhi pa/_/tsan+dan mchog sogs byugs nas bde ba'i stan/_/rdo rje skyil mo krung 'dug bsam gtan bya/_/dang por tho rangs nyi dbus su/_/steng 
[\[4a.6\]]sogs kyis dag mthing ga'i hU~M/_/'od zer gyis mthong yul dmigs te/_/snying rje la sogs bsgom par bya/_/'od zer gyis gsal sangs rgyas dang /_/bla ma la sogs mdun du dmigs/_/gzugs rdo rje la sogs pa yi/_/mchod pa'i sprin spro phyag kyang 'tshal/_/gang 
[\[4a.7\]]zhig thogs med dus nas srid pa'i chu rgyun la/_/rtog pas rnam par bsgos pa'i sdig pa ma lus rnams/_/thugs rje cher ldan khyod kyi mdun du yang /_/gsum du dmigs pa gsal te bshags par bgyi/_/sangs rgyas dang ni byang chub sems dpa' dang /_/'phags pa gzhan 
[\[4b\]]
[\[4b.1\]]rnams kyi yang dge ba gang /_/ma lus bdag gis de dag yi rangs nas/_/bla med yang dag byang chub phyir bsngo'o/_/spros med thugs ni zla ba lta bu ste/_/mtha' yas don du thabs kyi sku chen po/_/rtag tu bdag gi yid dang dbyer med pa/_/bde bar gshegs pa rtag tu skyabs 
[\[4b.2\]]su mchi/_/rtog pa kun las nges par grol ba yi/_/dam pa ma lus kun gyi phun tshogs rten/_/dngos po kun gyi ngo bo gcig pa po/_/chos la bdag ni rtag tu skyabs su mchi/_/bcings pa ma lus rnam par spangs pa yi/_/dpal ldan snying rje mchog gis rab drangs 
[\[4b.3\]]pa/_/rab tu dga' la sogs pa rab gnas pa'i/_/'dul ba'i dbang po'i tshogs la phyag 'tshal lo/_/bsam pa dag pas rnam smin dag pa yis/_/sgrib pa'i bag chags ma lus kun bcom ste/_/mi 'phrog pa yis brgyan pa'i 
[\[4b.4\]]sems kyis kyang /_/byang chub tu ni sems ni bskyed par bgyi/_/sras dang ldan pa'i bde bar gshegs pa yi/_/thun mong lam ste pha rol phyin pa bcu/_/rdzogs sangs rgyas kyi bdag nyid yid kyis ni/_/da ltar 'dir ni bdag gis bstan par bya/_/sdug bsngal 
[\[4b.5\]]la gnas sems can rnams/_/snying rjes yang ni dmigs byas te/_/byang chub sems ni dpag med pa/_/rin chen bdag gis bsam par bya/_/rang bzhin med phyir stong pa nyid/_/rgyu med pas na mtshan ma med/_/rjes su dpag pa dang bral bas/_/smon med nyid du nges 
[\[4b.6\]]pa'o/_/oM shU n+ya tA dz+nyA na badzra swa b+hA wa At+ma ko\u0f85 haM/_sngags des bdag gzhan dngos po kun/_/gcig byas de nyid gsum mtshan ma/_/dgyes mdzad yas mar sbrengs pa yi/_/mdun gyi mkha' la bsam par bya/_/stong las nyi dkyil hU~M nag las/_/rdo rje zer 'phros phyi 
[\[4b.7\]]rol bcad/_/'og dang phyogs mtshams steng spros pas/_/sa gzhi ra ba bla rer bskyed/_/b+h+rU~M 'khor rtsibs bcu 'od zer drag_/pad nyir khro bo 'khor lor bsgom/_/de dbus hU~M mdzad rtag pa sogs/_/gnas 'gyur de las chos 'byung bskyed/_/gru gsum dkar po 
[\[5a\]]
[\[5a.1\]]gyen la gnas/_/sna tshogs 'od zer phyogs bcur 'phro/_/nam mkha'i dkyil du pad+ma dang /_/sna tshogs rdo rje bsam par bya/_/de dbus zla steng b+h+rU~M las skyes/_/'khor lo b+h+rU~M mtshan las byung ba/_/rig ma dang bcas snang mdzad las/_/gyur 
[\[5a.2\]]pa'i khang pa brtsegs pa bsgom/_/gru bzhi la sogs yon tan ldan/_/sna tshogs pad+ma bcu dgu'i steng /_/nyi ma'i gdan ni bdun dag dang /_/zla ba'i gdan ni bcu gnyis bskyed/_/de nyid gsum gnas sems [(]pa bas [,]babs [)]pas/_/bde gshegs rnams kyis de la 
[\[5a.3\]]gzigs/_/rang gi khams su shes rab thabs/_/dbyangs yig bde ba'i yi ger 'gyur/_/dbu rtse las byon ye shes sems/_/'dres gyur rten babs zla gnyis gyur/_/'du zhing 'phro ba'i sbyor ba las/_/gcig tu gyur pa bsam pa'o/_/de las hU~M dkar 
[\[5a.4\]]las byung ba/_/rdo rje rtse lnga me shel gzugs/_/phyogs kun 'od zer 'phro bsams nas/_/hU~M bcas ldan pas nga rgyal bya/_/oM badzra At+ma ko\u0f85haM/_de las 'phros pa bde gshegs kyis/_/'gro ba sangs rgyas nyid du byas/_/slar 'dus thugs kyi rdo rje las/_/
[\[5a.5\]]btsud nas bdag la nga rgyal bya/_/oM ya thA sarba ta thA ga ta sta thA a haM/_sa bon gsang gsum rdo rjer gyur/_/gcig gyur rdo rje sems dpa' mchog_/'gro kun ro mnyam gcig pa po/_/kun du bzang po bsgom par bya/_/sku dang dbus zhal zla nor 'dra/_/g.yas gnag 
[\[5a.6\]]g.yon ni dmar po la/_/rin chen sna tshogs rgyan ldan pa'i/_/phyag gnyis rang snang shes rab 'khyud/_/g.yas gnyis rdo rje ral gri ste/_/g.yon gnyis rin chen pad+ma'o/_/mtshon byed mtshan ma dbu rgyan la/_/mi bskyod pa yis brgyan par bsgom/_/de nas 'dod 
[\[5a.7\]]chags chen po yis/_/'dus pa'i 'jig rten gzigs gyur nas/_/sbyor ba'i sgra yis rang lus la/_/rnam snang la sogs gzhug par bya/_/de ni spyan ma la sogs dang /_/gzugs la sogs pa yul gyi bdag_/rnam pa dgu ni spro byas nas/_/rang gi shes rab 
[\[5b\]]
[\[5b.1\]]nyid la thim/_/shes rab de yi pad dkyil du/_/bde las sprul pa'i 'khor lo bskyed/_/gzigs gyur 'gro ba ma lus pa/_/snod du rung phyir gzhug par bya/_/rang gi sa bon 'od zer gyis/_/nam mkha' khyab pa'i bde gshegs rnams/_/zla ba lta bur zhu byas 
[\[5b.2\]]pas/_/de rnams dag phyir dbang bskur bya/_/de yi stobs kyis de dag rnams/_/bde dang yid bde thob pa dang /_/tha mal phung sogs bshigs pa'i phyir/_/zla ba lta bur zhu ba'o/_/de la mig la sogs pa rnams/_/k+ShaM dzaM khaM gaM skaM saM gyur/_/de bzhin gzugs 
[\[5b.3\]]la sogs pa rnams/_/dzaHhU~M baM hoHkhaM raM gyur/_/sa la sogs pa'i rang bzhin dag_/laM maM paM taM du ni gyur/_/gzugs sogs phung po'i bdag nyid rnams/_/buM AM dzaM khaM hU~M du gyur/_/de las mtshan ma de yis mtshan/_/de rnams sa snying la sogs par/_/khams kun 
[\[5b.4\]]'das par byas nas su/_/dris shing rtogs phul mi smra 'dug_/de ni pad+ma'i gzhal yas nas/_/phyi rol phyung nas bstod nas ni/_/sna tshogs pa yi gzugs kyis so/_/rang rang khams su 'phros par bsam/_/de nas bar ma'i sems can rnams/_/skar ma lhung 
[\[5b.5\]]zhing zhal zhugs nas/_/rdo rjer phyung nas pad+mar gnas/_/shes rab chags pas de zhu byed/_/de yi 'od zer dmar 'phros pas/_/yab yum byang chub sems su zhu/_/de las byung ba'i spyan la sogs/_/glu yis bskul nas thim par byed/_/sems can khams na gnas kyi 
[\[5b.6\]]dbang phyug khyod ni rdo rje'i thugs/_/dga' ba'i yid 'ong don chen 'dod pa bdag la [(]bskab [,]bskyab [)]tu gsol/_/mgon po gal te de ring bdag ni gson par mchis bzhed na/_/sems can yab chen gnyen mchog bdag la dgyes par mdzad du gsol/_/rdo rje sku khyod 
[\[5b.7\]]bka' yi 'khor los sems can kun la phan/_/sangs rgyas don du byang chub mchog tu rab tu phan par gzigs/_/mgon po gal te bdag ni de ring gson par mchis bzhed na/_/spyod pa dri med kun du bzang pos bdag la dgyes par mdzod/_/khyod ni rdo rje gsung 
[\[6a\]]
[\[6a.1\]]ste kun la phan zhing thugs brtse ba/_/'jig rten dag gi dgos pa'i don mdzad pa la rtag tu brtson/_/mgon po gal te bdag ni de ring gson par mchis bzhed na/_/spyod pa dri med kun du bzang pos bdag la dgyes par mdzod/_/khyod ni rdo rje 
[\[6a.2\]]'dod pa dam tshig mchog gi phan yon che/_/yang dag rdzogs sangs rgyas kyi gdung mchog mnyam pa nyid gzigs la/_/mgon po gal te bdag ni de ring gson par mchis bzhed na/_/yon tan rin chen mang po'i gter gyur bdag la dgyes par mdzod/_/klu 'di 
[\[6a.3\]]yis ni don byas nas/_/zhu ba phun sum tshogs bral ba/_/zla nyir gyur te yi ge hUM/_/rdo rje sngon po cung zad gdengs/_/des mtshan bshes gnyen zhu byas pas/_/sku dang zhal dbus mthing ka'i mdog_/g.yas dkar g.yon ni dmar po la/_/dam pa dang 
[\[6a.4\]]po'i mtshan mar ldan/_/dam pa ma yin rnam rtog rnams/_/stong pa nyid du bsam byas te/_/mya ngan 'das pa zhen spang phyir/_/chos kyi dbyings su byin gyis brlabs/_/oM d+harma d+hA tu swa b+hA wa At+ma ko\u0f85haM/_sa snying rdo rje nam mkha' dang /_/dbang phyug sgrib 
[\[6a.5\]]sel kun bzang rnams/_/k+ShiM la sogs pa'i rim pa yis/_/mig la sogs par dgod par bya/_/de bzhin snying mgrin mgo zla la/_/hUM AHoM las skyes pa yi/_/lte bar thugs rdo rje sogs bskyed/_/de rnams thugs ka nas byung ba'i/_/gzugs kyi 
[\[6a.6\]]rdo rje sogs 'od kyis/_/ma lus rdo rje bsam mchod nas/_/byin gyis brlab phyir gsol ba btab/_/dpal ldan thugs ni rdo rje 'chang /_/rdo rje mi phyed gsum bsgoms pas/_/deng bdag byin gyis brlabs nas su/_/rdo rje thugs can mdzad du gsol/_/phyogs 
[\[6a.7\]]bcur bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams/_/rdo rje mi phyed gsum bsgoms pas/_/deng bdag byin gyis brlabs nas su/_/rdo rje thugs can mdzad du gsol/_/oM sarba ta thA ga ta tsit+ta badzra swa b+hA wa badzra At+ma ko\u0f85haM/_chos kyi gsung lam dpal dang ldan/_/rdo rje 
[\[6b\]]
[\[6b.1\]]mi phyed gsum bsgoms pas/_/deng bdag byin gyis brlabs nas su/_/rdo rje gsung dang 'dra bar mdzod/_/phyogs bcur bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams/_/rdo rje mi phyed gsum bsgoms pas/_/deng bdag byin gyis brlabs nas su/_/rdo rje gsung can mdzad du 
[\[6b.2\]]gsol/_/oM sarba ta thA ga ta wA ka badzra swa b+hA wa At+ma ko \u0f85haM/_dpal ldan sangs rgyas sku 'chang ba/_/rdo rje mi phyed gsum bsgoms pas/_/deng bdag byin gyis brlabs nas su/_/rdo rje sku dang ldan par mdzod/_/phyogs bcur bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams/_/
[\[6b.3\]]rdo rje mi phyed gsum bsgoms pas/_/deng bdag byin gyis brlabs nas su/_/rdo rje sku can mdzad du gsol/_/oM sarba ta thA ga ta kA ya badzra swa b+hA wa At+ma ko\u0f85 haM/_ye shes sems dpa'i sa bon gyi/_/'od kyis phyogs bcu'i mgon po bskul/_/de yi 'od zer las 
[\[6b.4\]]byung ba'i/_/spyan la sogs pa mchog rnams kyi/_/bdud rtsi lnga yis chus gang ba'i/_/bum pas dbang bskur bkra shis sgra/_/rnga dung dril sogs rol mo'i sgra/_/dbu rgyan rdo rje 'chang ldan bya/_/de bzhin zla ba gnyis la sogs/_/'gro ba'i dbang bskur ma 
[\[6b.5\]]gtogs pa/_/thams cad sngon bzhin bshes gnyen dang /_/gzugs kyi khyad par shes byas nas/_/byin gyis brlab cing mig la sogs/_/sa snying sogs bkod rdo rje gsum/_/gnas gsum mtshan las dbu rgyan la/_/rdo rje 'dzin pas mtshan pa'o/_/mgo 
[\[6b.6\]]snying lte ba gsang rkang par/_/oM hU~M swA a hA rnams sam/_/cha shas ngo bo nyid kyis ni/_/sangs rgyas lnga yis dgang bar bya/_/hU~M las rdo rje chu skyes ni/_/rtse lnga 'dab brgyad bsam par bya/_/oM las nor bu ge sar bskyed/_/phaT kyis gnyis kyi lam 'gogs pa'o/_/
[\[6b.7\]]mtshan nyid lnga dang ldan bsams nas/_/bde mchog myong bar bya ba'i phyir/_/rang gi shes rab la zhugs nas/_/dbang po gnyis sbyar rjes chags byas/_/oM sarba ta thA ga ta a nu rA ga badzra swa b+hA ba At+ma ko\u0f85haM/_sbyor ba dang po'i ting 'dzin no/_/thugs ka'i sa bon 'od lcags 
[\[7a\]]
[\[7a.1\]]kyus/_/sangs rgyas 'khor lo mkha' khyab pa/_/zhal zhugs zhu gyur rdo rjer phyung /_/pad gnas zla ba lta bu'i gzugs/_/de las sa bon hU~M bcu dgu/_/mi bskyod pa yi 'khor lo mchog_/gsal bar bsams nas 'gro ba kun/_/phan 
[\[7a.2\]]phyir rim gyis dbyung bar bya/_/badzra d+h+r-ika gis rab bskul la/_/gong ma lta bu'i mi bskyod pa/_/sangs rgyas mdzad pa byas nas su/_/'dus nas bdag la zhugs par bsam/_/dzi ni dzika gis rnam snang dbyung /_/dkar nag dmar po phyag drug pa/_/'khor lo 
[\[7a.3\]]ral gri rin chen pad/_/'dus nas shar gyi zla bar bsam/_/rat+na d+h+r-ika gis rin chen dbyung /_/ser nag dkar po phyag drug pa/_/rin chen ral gri 'khor lo pad/_/'dus nas lho yi nyi mar bsam/_/A ro l-ika gis 'od dpag med/_/dmar nag dkar po 
[\[7a.4\]]phyag drug pa/_/pad+ma ral gri rin chen 'khor/_/slar 'dus nub kyi nyi mar bsam/_/pradz+nyA d+h+r-ika gis don yod grub/_/ljang nag dkar po phyag drug pa/_/ral gri 'khor lo rin chen pad/_/'dus nas byang gi nyi mar bsam/_/dang po rang gi rig ma 'khyud/_/rgyan 
[\[7a.5\]]kun brgyan pa'i na bza' dang /_/rin chen sna tshogs dbu rgyan can/_/gdan ltar 'od kyi dkyil 'khor lo/_/mo ha ra tis spyan ma dbyung /_/sku yi rdo rje dang 'dra ba/_/'khor lo ral gri rin chen pad/_/slar 'dus shar lhor zla bar bsam/_/dwe Sha ra tis mA ma kI/_/
[\[7a.6\]]thugs kyi rdo rje dang 'dra ba/_/ut+pal ral gri rin chen pad/_/'dus nas lho nub zla bar bsam/_/rA ga ra tis gos dkar mo/_/gsung gi rdo rje dang 'dra ba/_/pad ral gri rin chen 'khor/_/'dus nas nub byang zla bar bsam/_/badzra ra tis sgrol ma dbyung /_/rin 
[\[7a.7\]]chen dbang phyug dang 'dra ba/_/ut+pal 'khor lo rin chen pad/_/slar 'dus byang shar zla bar bsam/_/oM AHhUM gis lha mo drug_/[{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du dbyung bar bya/_/gzugs kyi rdo rje rnam snang 'dra/_/me long ral gri rin chen pad/_/sgra yi rdo rje mi bskyod 'dra/_/
[\[7b\]]
[\[7b.1\]]pi waM ral gri rin chen pad/_/dri yi rdo rje rin chen 'dra/_/dung dang ral gri rin chen pad/_/ro yi rdo rje mtha' yas 'dra/_/snod dang ral gri rin chen 'khor/_/reg gi rdo rje don yod 'dra/_/gos dang rin chen 'khor lo pad/_/chos dbyings rdo rje sems dpa' 'dra/_/chos 
[\[7b.2\]]'byung ral gri rin chen pad/_/me mtshams la sogs grwa bzhi dang /_/shar sgo g.yas g.yon zla steng bsam/_/ya mAn+ta kr-ita gshin rje'i gshed/_/mi bskyod lta bu phyag drug pa/_/tho ba ral gri rin chen pad/_/shar sgor khro dkyil nyi dbus dgod/_/pradz+nyAn+ta kr-ita 
[\[7b.3\]]shes mthar byed/_/snang mdzad lta bu phyag drug pa/_/dbyug pa ral gri rin chen pad/_/lho sgor nyi dbus nyi 'od 'bar/_/pad+man+ta kr-ita rta mgrin ni/_/mtha' yas lta bu phyag drug pa/_/pad+ma rin chen ral gri 'khor/_/nub sgor nyi dkyil khro dkyil bsam/_/big+h+nan+ta kr-ita 
[\[7b.4\]]bdud rtsi 'khyil/_/mi bskyod lta bu phyag drug pa/_/rdo rje ral gri rin chen pad/_/byang sgor nyi dbus khro dkyil bsam/_/de rnams rgod dang gdangs pa dang /_/mche ba gtsigs dang bzhad byed pa/_/dbu skra dmar ser gyen du [(]brjes[,]brdzes[)]/_/stag lpags smad g.yogs dang ldan 
[\[7b.5\]]pa/_/gtum pa'i sbrul gyis brgyan pa yi/_/dang pos rig ma 'khyud pa dang /_/me yi 'od kyi 'khor lo nang /_/gtum po rab tu bsam pa'o/_/de ltar dngos po thams cad kyis/_/rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor mthong nas su/_/sa bon 'od zer lcags kyu yis/_/gshin rje 
[\[7b.6\]]gshed bskul 'khor lo dgug_/de dang lhan cig 'ongs pa'i bgegs/_/bskrad nas bsrung byas khro bo thim/_/bde bas rgyas pa'i dkyil 'khor ni/_/mdun du dmigs la mchod par bya/_/dung gi chu yis bsang gtor dang /_/kun das kha bkang chus nye reg_/mchod 
[\[7b.7\]]yon snod kyis mchod yon dang /_/mtha' sogs dbye bas zhabs bsil lo/_/de bzhin khro bo gzhan bskul bas/_/'od zer gyis ni gzhug pa dang /_/bcing dang rjes su dgyes bskyed nga /_/chos kyi dbyings su byin gyis brlab/_/mos pas mig la sogs pa rnams/_/
[\[8a\]]
[\[8a.1\]]rdo rje gsum ni gnas gsum bkod/_/snga ma bzhin du dbang bskur bas/_/rigs kyi dbang bskur gzhag pa'o/_/sangs rgyas bzhi po mi bskyod pa/_/lha mo rang rang bdag pos brgyan/_/rnam snang mi bskyod snang mtha' yas/_/mi bskyod sgo srung 
[\[8a.2\]]mtshan ma'o/_/sa bon 'od kyis lha mo bkug_/zhal zhugs zhu gyur rdo rjer phyung /_/pad nang laM maM paM taM dang /_/dzaHhU~M baM hoHkhaM raM gyur/_/de yi 'od kyis bcu po dgug_/rang rang sa bon la zhugs pas/_/nus dang ldan par rab byas nas/_/shes rab ba spu'i bu gar spro/_/
[\[8a.3\]]'od rtse gzugs rdo rje sogs ni/_/rang rang mtshan mas rnam mdzes pa'i/_/dri med rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i sku/_/sgyu ma lta bur spro bar bya/_/de bzhin phyi yi gdugs la sogs/_/'od rtse las spro nam mkha'i khams/_/bkang ste bdag dang 'khor lo la/_/mchod par byas te 
[\[8a.4\]]mchod pa dang /_/mchod pa po dang mchod gnas gsum/_/rmi lam lta bur shes bya'i phyir/_/tha dad las ni grol byas nas/_/sngags kyis byin gyis brlab par bya/_/oM sarba ta thA ga ta pU dza badzra swa b+hA wa At+ma ko\u0f85haM/_de nas brag ca'i sgra lta bur/_/sgra yul thams cad 
[\[8a.5\]]rab bsams nas/_/rdo rje dril bu bde dbyangs kyis/_/rang nyid lha yis bstod par bsgom/_/mi bskyod rdo rje ye shes che/_/rdo rje dbyings ni mkha' chen po/_/rdo rje gsum mchog dkyil 'khor gsum/_/gsang ba'i dbyings la phyag 'tshal bstod/_/rnam par snang mdzad 
[\[8a.6\]]dga' chen po/_/rdo rje zhi ba dga' ba che/_/rang bzhin gyis ni 'od gsal mchog_/ston pa rdo rje phyag 'tshal bstod/_/rin chen rgyal po rab tu zab/_/rdo rje nam mkha' dri ma med/_/dngos nyid dag pas gos pa med/_/rdo rje sku la phyag 'tshal bstod/_/rdo rje dpag med rgyal po che/_/
[\[8a.7\]]mi rtogs nam mkha' rdo rje 'dzin/_/'dod chags pha rol phyin pa brnyes/_/rdo rje gsung la phyag 'tshal bstod/_/dag pa rdo rje gdon mi za/_/bsam pa thams cad yongs rdzogs pa/_/rang bzhin dag pa nyid las byung /_/rdo rje sems dpa' phyag 'tshal bstod/_/rlung dang 
[\[8b\]]
[\[8b.1\]]me yi dkyil 'khor nas/_/pad+ma b+han+d+har bdud rtsi lnga/_/zla ba nyi mar yi ge lnga/_/bsams nas 'od zer spro bya'o/_/rtag sogs bkug nas sa bon dgug_/bzhu zhing bskol bas rlangs nas hU~M/_/de byung rdo rje zhu byas nas/_/phreng ba'i rgyun du dbab par bya/_/oM gyis 
[\[8b.2\]]bsil bar byas nas su/_/lha rnams phyag tu spros byas la/_/lce hU~M las byung rdo rje bkod/_/snying zla'i 'khor lo tshim par bya/_/dkyil 'khor rgyal ba mchog ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to/_/ye shes sems dpa'i thugs 'od kyis/_/thub pa'i dbang pos kun 
[\[8b.3\]]rtog spangs/_/'gro kun sangs rgyas nyid byas nas/_/sa bon la ni thim par bya/_/thugs ka'i mtshan ma nang gnas pa/_/nyi ma'i dbus kyi yi ge hU~M/_/'od zer rnam pa lnga ldan pa'i/_/tsa na'i 'bru tsam thig ler bsam/_/de yi 'od kyis gnas la sogs/_/rdo rje 
[\[8b.4\]]mchog gsum gsal byas nas/_/bzhag ste thig le lta bu ru/_/rang gi sems nyid zhugs par bya/_/de ma thag tu ting 'dzin gsum/_/grub pa rab tu bsam byas nas/_/de yi gsum pa las spro zhing /_/thig le yan chad gsal bar bya/_/gzhan pa 
[\[8b.5\]]ba spu las spro zhing /_/dkyil 'khor rten la sogs pa nas/_/khams kun bkang nas mtha' la bsdu/_/de rim yang ni spro bsdu bya/_/yang ni sa bon 'od kyis ni/_/bde gshegs dgug gzhug zhu gyur nas/_/rdo rje'i lam nas phyung nas su/_/ze'u 'bru bum 
[\[8b.6\]]pa'i nang du dbab/_/de las rdo rje mthing ka che/_/nas tshad lte bar rin po che/_/yungs kar tshad tsam dbus su yang /_/rten bcas ting 'dzin gsum po bsgom/_/rdo rje rtse lnga lcags kyu yis/_/phyung ste sna rtser gnas par bya/_/rdo rje nang nas thur sel rlung /_/phyung ste 
[\[8b.7\]]gnas bya rnam pa gzhan/_/'di yi ting 'dzin gsum pa las/_/spro bsdu gong ma bzhin du bya/_/de la bzung bas stobs ldan na/_/brtan pa'i mtshan ma skye bar 'gyur/_/de skyes sprul pa 'phro ba ni/_/sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang /_/rnam pa sna tshogs phyag mtshan 
[\[9a\]]
[\[9a.1\]]rnams/_/yang nas yang du spro bsdu bya/_/sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje dang /_/ye shes sems dpa'i sa bon las/_/gsang sngags bzlas brjod spro byed pa/_/rim pa'am ni cig car bya/_/oM sogs re re brjod skad cig_/sku rdo rje la sogs 'khor 
[\[9a.2\]]lo/_/spros pas nam mkha'i khams bkang nas/_/yan lag bcu gnyis gsum ldan mos/_/oM sogs re re brjod skad cig_/ma bskul gnyis dang gong ma ltar/_/dang pos phyung ste nam mkha' la/_/bkod nas gnyis pa gong ma ltar/_/mchod pa'i lha mo spro byas nas/_/
[\[9a.3\]]'khyud pa'i bde bas thub dbang rnams/_/bde chen dbang du gyur byas la/_/gsum pas nang du thim par bya/_/rdo rje dang ni gsal ba dang /_/lam byung chos kyi 'khor lo las/_/steng du g.yas g.yon gnyi ga las/_/drag dang dal bar shes par bya/_/'byung ba hU~M ste 'jug pa oM/_/
[\[9a.4\]]gnas pa AHste de nyid bzung /_/ye shes sa bon ngo bo ste/_/gzhi rnams gcig tu brtsi bar bya/_/nyis brgya nyi shu rtsa lnga las/_/bzhi bsdus brgya phrag dgu nyid do/_/de ni nyi shu rtsa bzhi rim/_/nyi khri chig stong drug brgya'o/_/phra myur rnams kyis ngal ba bsal/_/spyan sogs glu 
[\[9a.5\]]yis bskul byas nas/_/sngon bzhin 'khor lo mchod bya ste/_/sems kyi gzengs ni bstod bya ste/_/nyam chung spyi bor mtho gang tshad/_/zla steng oM gyi brlan pa yi/_/bdud rtsi cho ga bzhin phab pas/_/rdo rje gsum sogs tshim par bya/_/baM las byung ba'i bum pa bzang /_/zla 
[\[9a.6\]]gam phyed kyis kha bcad pa/_/thigs pa yid bzhin rin po ches/_/brgyan pa rkan gyi dbus su bsgom/_/'od zer lnga ni rab 'bar ba'i/_/oM ni lce yi rtse la bsam/_/de gzer rim pas dbab byas pas/_/nus dang ldan pas bkres skom gzhom/_/bkres skom la sogs 
[\[9a.7\]]nyon mongs la/_/rnal 'byor de ni kun brtsams nas/_/rdo rje thugs kyi bka' bzhin du/_/sdug bsngal thams cad 'byang bar 'gyur/_/cung zad ngal sos yul gzhan 'gro/_/gong mi'i cho gas brtan bya'i phyir/_/rdo rje gsum sngags ma zhen par/_/gsang sngags bzlas 
[\[9b\]]
[\[9b.1\]]brjod yang bya'o/_/ldang bar 'dod na sngags pa yis/_/mchod bstod bdud rtsi myang byas la/_/oM AHhU~M nyid brjod byas la/_/de nyid gsum la 'khor lo bsdu/_/las kyi rgyal ba mchog ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to/_/dpal ldan gsang ba 'dus pa yi/_/rig pa'i 
[\[9b.2\]]lta ba ma thob pa'i/_/sems can thugs rjes gzung ba'i phyir/_/smon lam 'di ni sngon du bya/_/dpal dang ldan pa'i mi bskyod mgon/_/rnal 'byor gyis thob dge ba gang /_/bde chen ye shes rang byung nas/_/sems can kun mkhyen sar 'god shog_/gang zhig 'di 
[\[9b.3\]]mthong 'dod pa'am/_/gang zhig zhu bar 'dod pa des/_/thugs kyi dbang phyug grangs med pa/_/mgon po de ni mthong bar shog_/nam mkha' mtha' klas phyogs bcu ru/_/sems can kun gyi don spyod la/_/ji ltar thugs kyi dbang phyug bzhin/_/bdag gis kyang 
[\[9b.4\]]ni spyod par shog_/ji srid thog ma med pa nas/_/'khor ba yi ni mthar thug par/_/de srid sems can phan pa nyid/_/sangs rgyas spyod pa bdag spyod shog_/ji srid sangs rgyas sna tshogs rnams/_/'jig rten bshes gnyen mya ngan 'das/_/de yi bstan pa'i don du ni/_/
[\[9b.5\]]bskal pa'i bskal par byed par shog_/rdo rje 'chang sogs gzugs kyis ni/_/bdag gis phyogs bcur sprul gyur cig_/sngags kyi tshul gyis sems can rnams/_/de tshe bdag gis 'dul gyur cig_/ting 'dzin las ni langs nas ni/_/lha yi nga rgyal dang 
[\[9b.6\]]ldan pas/_/spyod yul sems tsam shes byas la/_/rang gi lha yi gzugs su dmigs/_/yang na spyod yul de dag nyid/_/rang rang dag bas gnas pa'o/_/so so rang rig chos nyid pas/_/rnam snang mdzad la sogs par snang /_/yang na rdo rje 'dzin pa ste/_/rang 
[\[9b.7\]]yid dga' bar byed pa'i phyir/_/dbugs rtog spangs nas rab bsgoms nas/_/gsang ba chen po mchod pa'o/_/rang gi kha zas za dus su/_/snying khar 'khor lo rab bsgoms la/_/bdud rtsi sha yi sbyor ba yis/_/gong ma lta bur sbyang byas te/_/de nyid gsum ni 
[\[10a\]]
[\[10a.1\]]bzlas byas te/_/lce dang dbu rgyan mchog bsgom la/_/lha rnams ma lus tshim pa yi/_/bsam pas nang gi sbyin sreg bya/_/phyi rol sbyin sreg dpag pa tsam/_/ye shes me ni 'bar byed pas/_/phung sogs blun pa'i bud shing bsreg_/bla na med par 
[\[10a.2\]]bshad pa'o/_/ji skad gsungs pa'i sdom par ldan/_/ji ltar myong bar mnyam par spyod/_/blo dang ldan pas dam med la/_/dus rig byas la gsang bar bya/_/snying rjer gnas pas las gnyis byed/_/'gro ba'i don dang sangs rgyas mnyes/_/lus ngag tha mal 
[\[10a.3\]]khyad par can/_/phyag rgya sngags kyi bdag nyid do/_/chos can gcig la rtog pa ni/_/sna tshogs rnam par snang ba yin/_/sdug bsngal bde ba grol 'byung ba/_/las kyi khyad par mang gyur yin/_/de phyir dge dang mi dge'i las/_/lus la sogs pa'i rtog las 
[\[10a.4\]]byung /_/kun rtog thams cad las grol ba/_/nag po'i las su ga la 'gyur/_/dam tshig nyams pa bskang don du/_/don yod rdo rje sbyor bsgom ste/_/thugs ka'i zla bar khaM bskyed pa'i/_/sna tshogs rdo rje bsam par bya/_/gzugs sogs nam mkha' bzhin dag nas/_/
[\[10a.5\]]rang bzhin 'od gsal sems bskyed de/_/bskul ba sngon du 'gro ba yis/_/dbang bskur byas pas dag par bya/_/bla ma brnyas pa'i skyon byung na/_/yon dang ldan pa'i ston mo dbul/_/sdig bshags la sogs sngon 'gro ba'i/_/sdom pa bzung bas bskang bar bya/_/
[\[10a.6\]]phyi yi khrus kyi cho ga la/_/rang gi rnal 'byor dmigs byas nas/_/dbang bskur gong ma bzhin bsgoms nas/_/mtha' ru bdag po gzhag pa'o/_/de ltar nyin phyed dus dang ni/_/phyi 'bred du yang bsgom par bya/_/nam phyed dus su sa bon nas/_/bzlas pa'i dus su 
[\[10a.7\]]bskyed par bya/_/gshegs su gsol 'dod sngags pa yis/_/mchod pa bstod pa bdud rtsi myang /_/byas nas mtha' ru mu bzhag pa'i/_/yi ge gsum gyis gshegs su gsol/_/de bzhin yi ge gsum brjod pas/_/ye shes sems dpa'i 'khor lo la/_/phyi 
[\[10b\]]
[\[10b.1\]]rol 'khor lo bcug byas te/_/shes rab snying rje ldan pas nyal/_/ldang ba'i dus na lha mo yi/_/dbyangs kyis bskul bsam langs nas su/_/sngon bzhin dus bzhir ting 'dzin gsum/_/rtse gcig pa yis thams cad bsgom/_/brel ba bcad pa'i rnal 'byor 
[\[10b.2\]]pas/_/dus dang por ni ting 'dzin gsum/_/ma lus par ni bsgom byas la/_/yan lag rnams ni sngon bzhin bsam/_/de las gzhan pa'i dus dag tu/_/glo bur rang gi lha bzhengs la/_/thugs las 'khor lo spro byas la/_/dbang bskur ba ni sngon bzhin blang /_/
[\[10b.3\]]mchod bstod bdud rtsi myang byas la/_/thig le 'am yang na phra mo'am/_/sngags kyi bzlas brjod yang byas nas/_/'khor lo bsdu ba sngon bzhin brtsam/_/de ltar zhugs pa rnam pa gnyis/_/rang gi lam gyi mi mthun phyogs/_/bzlog pas brtan pa'i rtags rnyed pas/_/
[\[10b.4\]]las dang po yi rnal 'byor ro/_/de yis sku la dmigs pa yis/_/man ngag thob pas ye shes che/_/'bebs thob rang dang gzhan mthong na/_/cung zad ye shes dbang gyur pa'o/_/dus bzhir mig dang lus sogs rnams/_/byin rlabs dbang bskur rab byas nas/_/
[\[10b.5\]]mchod pa sogs kyis mnyes byas la/_/bzlas brjod sbyor ba brtsam pa'o/_/sngon bzhin du ni bsdu byas nas/_/sdom pa bskyang sogs snga ma bzhin/_/rgyun du goms rlabs bcu dang gcig_/mthu chen stobs rtogs des dmigs so/_/de yi gsung la dmigs pa yis/_/
[\[10b.6\]]rlung gi rang bzhin rtogs mdzad nas/_/bdag dang gzhan gyis dkyil 'khor mthong /_/ye shes dbang du gyur pa'o/_/de la nyin mtshan rgyun du'am/_/yi ge gsum gyi ting nge 'dzin/_/kun du spyod las chos thams cad/_/dbyer med gong bzhin snyen byed do/_/'di yi phyag 
[\[10b.7\]]rgya chen po yi/_/bsgrub pa'i mtshan ma gsum mthong nas/_/brtul zhugs la sogs rim 'di yis/_/sems dmigs rdo rje 'chang ba thob/_/'di las gzhan pa bcu bzhi yi/_/bsnyen pa'i cho ga 'di bzhin te/_/rang rang lha yi sbyor ba yis/_/'og nas 'byung ba'i 
[\[11a\]]
[\[11a.1\]]don brtson bya/_/de yan chad ni sngon du bsnyen pa bya ba'i cho ga rgyas par bshad zin to//_//da ni slob ma rnams la dbang bskur ba'i cho ga bshad par bya'o/_/de la gang gis dbang bskur zhe na/_dang por zhugs dang dang po pa/_/thams cad 
[\[11a.2\]]kun gyi slob dpon no/_/cung zad ye shes dbang gyur pa/_/nus dang ldan pa'i slob dpon no/_/ye shes dbang du gyur pa ni/_/sbyor ba rnyed pa'i slob dpon no/_/de rnams ci rigs mnyes byas nas/_/de yis dkyil 'khor brtsam pa'o/_/gang dag gi don du zhe na/_/dad ldan 
[\[11a.3\]]gtong phod brtson 'grus ldan/_/snod rung srog la mi lta ba/_/tshe 'dir dngos grub thob 'dod pas/_/gnyis sam gsum gyi bar du zhus/_/sku gzugs bris dang lder so dang /_/glegs bam rdo rje la sogs dang /_/mchod rten la sogs gnas pa'i phyir/_/gnyis 
[\[11a.4\]]sam gsum gyi bar du zhus/_/gang dag sdig pa'i las dbang gis/_/mngon du'am rmi lam dag tu ni/_/lha 'khrug pa yis mtshan ma mthong /_/gnyis sam gsum gyi bar du zhus/_/mos dang brtan la dad pa can/_/gzhan la phan dga' mthong nas ni/_/ma zhus kyang ni 
[\[11a.5\]]de bos la/_/phan 'dod phyir ni dkyil 'khor brtsam/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_bum pa'i dbang gis rab sbyor bas/_/sa brgyad pa ru yang dag gnas/_/gang dag gsang ba'i dbang gis ni/_/dgu pa dang ni bcu par byed/_/shes rab ye shes dbang gis ni/_/rdo rje lta bur 
[\[11a.6\]]rab gnas pa'i/_/'bras bu 'byung bar nus mthu ldan/_/dang po bzhi par shes par bya/_/rab gnas ma byas gzugs brnyan la/_/thal bar brlag kyang 'bras bu med/_/sngags dang bral ba'i sbyin sreg bzhin/_/lus nyon mongs pa kho nar zad/_/de bas ji srid

[\[11b\]]
[\[11b.1\]]nas/_/ye shes kyis kyang 'debs pa yi/_/cho ga zab mos sdig pa zad/_/mtshan ma'i 'bras bu mi 'byung zhing /_/gong gi yon tan ldan par mdzad/_/thabs ji ltar zhe na/_bdag nyid chen po de la gong du smos pas zhu ba gsol ba dang /_khyod kyis spro bya rnams

[\[15a\]]
[\[15a.1\]]pa gsum byas la/_/'khyil pa'i sngags bzlas pho yi g.yon/_/mo yi dpung pa g.yas pa ru/_/bsrung ba bya phyir bcing bar bya/_/'khyil bzlas chu gtsang skyor chu yis/_/kha bshal lan gsum 'thung bcug ste/_/byin chags gtsang bar

[\[15a.3\]]kun gyi dam tshig dang /_/gsang ba mchog gi sdom pa rnams/_/rtag tu dbang gi slob dpon bgyid/_/sems can kun don byed phyir stsol/_/de nas bla mas thun mong sdom/_/byin nas slob ma de yis kyang /_/rig dbang thob phyir lan gsum du/_/bka' stsal de ni blang 
[\[15a.4\]]bya'o/_/kun mkhyen gang du 'jig rten du/_/bde bar 'byung ngam mi 'byung ba/_/u dum bA ra'i me tog bzhin/_/gal te brgya lam brgya 'ga' 'byung /_/bde bas thob dka' 'byung ba ni/_/gsang sngags spyod pa'i tshul 'di ni/_/ci phyir dpag med sems can don/_/bla na med pa

[\[15b\]]

[\[15b.3\]]'khor dmigs/_/mchod bstod bdud rtsi myang byas te/_/bsdu zhing ye shes 'khor lo ni/_/kha sbyar dbus su mkha' la bzhag_/tho rangs langs nas shar bltas te/_/so shing 'chos nas steng du bsgyur/_/ba lang ko tsam maN+Dal la/_/zhi ba la sogs grub par lta/_/
[\[15b.4\]]sbyin dang srung ba la sogs la/_/'khyil pa'i sngags ni mchog nyid las/_/mthong ba'i rmi lam mi dge bas/_/rang ngam des bzlas gzhom par bya/_/mtshan mo gsum pa'i cha dang por/_/skud pa brtag pa la sogs 'dra/_/de yi sa bon las byung ba/_/nyi shu rtsa 
[\[15b.5\]]lngar mos pa'o/_/mi bskyod rdo rje mig gnyis kyang /_/dza la nyi zlar bskyed des mtshan/_/rdzi ma'i rba rlabs lcags kyu yis/_/mkha' yi 'khor lo las dgug bya/_/oM AHrnam par snang mdzad che/_/rang gi 'khor lo la mchod phyir/_/khyed kyis skud pa bdag la stsol/_/
[\[15b.6\]]dip+ta driSh+Ta badzra aM gu sha dza hU~M/_de ltar mi bskyod par byas pas/_/rang rang skud pa rang la thim/_/so so gnas pa'i skud pa kun/_/chos nyid sngags kyis bsgril bar bya/_/sarba d+harma [(]spa [,]s+pha [)]ra Na a nu pra biSh+Ta sarba d+harma at+yan+ta a nu pra biSh+Ta d+harma/_dkyil 'khor nyis 'gyur ring thung 
[\[15b.7\]]dang /_/sgo yi nyi shu chas sbom phra/_/tsan+dan mchog sogs dri zhim gyis/_/byugs nas gser dngul snod du bsrung /_/yi ge gsum gyi bar bsams la/_/khrus byas rang lha'i nga rgyal gyis/_/de nas skud pa bsdogs byas nas/_/'khor lo thig ni gdab par bya/_/
[\[16a\]]
[\[16a.1\]]bdag nyid mi bskyod shar bltas nas/_/slob ma snang mdzad bdud rtsi 'khyil/_/lte bar khu tshur g.yon bzung nas/_/nam mkha'i thig ni ma lus gdab/_/nam mkha' la gnas de bzhin gshegs/_/sems can don byed dus bab pas/_/gnas 'dir 
[\[16a.2\]]byon pa'i don ched du/_/thig gi sgra yis bskul bar bya/_/oM badzra sa ma ya sU tra mA ti ya ma hU~M/_gnod sbyin gshin rje brgya byin glu/_/phyogs su kha bltas phyogs bzhir gdab/_/me mtshams bden bral mtshams gnas nas/_/grwa yi thig ni rab tu gdab/_/de nas tshangs 
[\[16a.3\]]thig sogs gcig tu/_/sum char byas la mtha' gnyis dor/_/dang po cha bzhir rab byas la/_/de yi dang po cha gnyis so/_/de yi gnyis pa gnyis pa bya/_/re re yang ni gnyis gnyis so/_/gsum pa'i dang pos cha chung gnyis/_/de nas yang ni cha chung gcig_/de nas 
[\[16a.4\]]cha chung gnyis dor nas/_/cha chen gcig ni dor byas nas/_/cha chung gcig ni bcad pa'o/_/yang ni gnyis dor gcig bcad do/_/gnyis dor gcig bcad de steng du/_/cha chung gnyis ni dor bar bya/_/de nas cha chen gsum ni bcad/_/de steng cha chung gsum dor nas/_/
[\[16a.5\]]yang ni cha chung gsum dor bya/_/lhag ma cha chung gcig lus pa'o/_/de nas thig skud gnyis 'gyur byas/_/tsan+dan phur bus dbus su gzugs/_/yang ni rang gi ra ba dang /_/phyi rol ra ba kun bskor bya/_/cha chen lnga ni rab bskor na/_/nang gi rdo rje ra ba ste/_/lha yi gnas 
[\[16a.6\]]la cha chung lnga/_/byas pa'i phyed gnyis ka bar 'gyur/_/phyi yi 'khor lo cha brgyad la/_/cha gcig sgo yi dbus bya'o/_/de tshad lha yi snam bu ste/_/de phyed 'khor sa chung ngu'o/_/de yi tshad kyi kha khyer sa/_/dra ba dra ba phyed pa dang /_/phreng bshal 'phyang ba'i snam bu dang /_/de bzhin 'gram pa 
[\[16a.7\]]gshog pa'o/_/logs kyi phyi rol ya phugs te/_/de ltar ka ba la yang ngo /_/de phyed rin chen pha gu dang /_/tshon sna lnga yi sa mnyam mo/_/de bzhin 'khor lo ka sogs dang /_/rdo rje ra ba'ang de bzhin no/_/ya phugs steng du cha chen gsum/_/ta ra Na yi sa ru 'gyur/_/cha chung drug la 
[\[16b\]]
[\[16b.1\]]pad+ma dang /_/rdo rje ra ba bskor bar bya/_/cha chen gcig ni 'od dkyil 'khor/_/bskor nas gcig ni nye 'khor las/_/ltas ngan gyis ni 'grub mi 'gyur/_/thig skud chad na slob dpon 'chi/_/lhag chad byung na 'bras sogs 'byung /_/phyogs 'khrul slob ma smyo bar 'gyur/_/gru bzhi 
[\[16b.2\]]sgo bzhi rta babs bzhis/_/brgyan cing dar sham me tog dang /_/'phan dang rgyal mtshan ba dan gyis/_/brgyan pa'i 'khor lo mthong bar bya/_/grwa yi cha ni thams cad dang /_/sgo yi zur kun cha la yang /_/zla ba rin chen rdo rje dgod/_/gam pa'i ri sogs rab brgyan pa/_/chos kyi 'khor lo 
[\[16b.3\]]ri dwags gnyis/_/'phan sogs kyis brgyan twa ra Na/_/ji ltar mdzes shing bklubs par bya/_/sems can rnams kyi bsam dbang gi/_/'di ni thugs kyi dkyil 'khor bas/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi rgyud 'di ru/_/khru ni bcu gnyis nyid du gsungs/_/de la khru bcu gnyis ni byang chub 
[\[16b.4\]]kyi sems la dga' ba'i rdo rje sgeg mo dang /_gzugs dang yon tan gyi rdo rje phreng ba ma dang /_chos 'chad pa'i rang bzhin rdo rje glu ma dang /_sems can thams cad kyi don byed pa'i rdo rje gar ma dang /_grags pa thams cad 'dus pa'i rdo rje bdug pa ma dang /_byang chub kyi yan lag gi rdo rje me tog ma 
[\[16b.5\]]dang /_'gro ba gzhan dga' bar byed pa'i rdo rje mar me ma dang /_tshul khrims la sogs pa'i rdo rje dri chab ma dang /_sbyin pa'i rang bzhin rdo rje kyo ba ma dang /_snyan par smra ba'i rang bzhin rdo rje gsol ka ma dang /_don mthun pa'i rang bzhin rdo rje pho nya ma dang /_don spyod pa'i rang bzhin rdo rje 'bebs
