[\[16b.7\]]cad mnyes par byed pa'i yon tan rdo rje chags pa dang /_gzi brjid bstod cing dga' ba'i yon tan rdo rje legs pa dang /_chos kyi rgyal por dbang bskur ba'i rdo rje rin chen dang /_gzi brjid gzhan las 'phags pa'i yon tan rdo rje gzi brjid dang /_re ba yongs su rdzogs pa'i yon tan rdo rje 
[\[17a\]]
[\[17a.1\]]dpal rab dang /[ (]mdzum [,]'dzum [)]pas lung ston pa'i yon tan rdo rje bzhad pa dang /_chos la dmigs shing so sor rtog pa'i yon tan rdo rje chos rab dang /_sgrib pa yongs su gcod pa'i yon tan rdo rje rnon po dang /_sku gsung thugs zad mi shes par kun la khyab pa'i yon 
[\[17a.2\]]tan rdo rje rgyu dang /_gzhung gi chos rab tu sgrogs pa'i yon tan rdo rje smra ba dang /_sems can smin pa dang grol bar byed pa'i yon tan rdo rje las rab dang /_mi 'jigs pa sbyin zhing srung ba'i rang bzhin rdo rje srung ba dang /_gdul dka' 'dul bar byed pa'i yon tan rdo rje gnod sbyin dang /_rdo rje gsum 
[\[17a.3\]]bsdus shing dbyer med pa'i yon tan rdo rje sdud pa ste/_de rnams rnam par dag pa'o/_/gsung gi dkyil 'khor la khru nyi shu gsungs pa ni/_de rnams dang rdo rje'i rigs rnams sdud cing dam tshig gi phyag rgya byin gyis rlob pa'i yon tan sems ma rdo rje ma dang /_rin po che'i rigs sdud cing dbang 
[\[17a.4\]]bskur ba'i byin gyis rlob pa'i rang bzhin yon tan rin chen rdo rje ma dang /pad+ma'i rigs sdud cing chos kyi phyag rgya byin gyis rlob pa'i yon tan chos kyi rdo rje ma dang /_las kyi rigs sdud cing las kyi phyag rgya byin gyis rlob pa'i yon tan las kyi rdo rje ma ste/_de rnams rnam par dag pa'o/_/ci'i phyir 
[\[17a.5\]]zhe na/_de rnams sa bcu rnam par dag pa ste/_sa bcu ni ye shes lnga'i rten du gyur pa'i phyir ro/_/ji skad du gsang ba'i thig le nor bu'i rgyud phyi ma las/_'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi/_/lam rnams dag ste rim pa bzhin/_/bcu gnyis bcu drug nyi shu ru/_/ye shes la ni ye shes 
[\[17a.6\]]gnas/_/thos dang bsam pa ngo bo ni/_/kyo ba la sogs rang bzhin no/_/shes rab brtson 'grus ting nge 'dzin/_/thar chags sdug pa la sogs so/_/drod la sogs pa dang por sbyor/_/sgeg mo la sogs ngo bo'o/_/lhag ma nyi shu gnyis gnyis kyang /_/sa re ru ni rnam dag pas/_/
[\[17a.7\]]ye shes rten du yang dag mtshams/_/stegs bu 'di yang 'khor lo yi/_/rten du yang dag gyur pas na/_/rnam par dag pa mngon du bya'o/_/zhes so/_/mdor na zab gsal gnyis med las/_/grub pa'i khyad par 'byung bas na/_/rigs dang tshad sogs nges med pas/_/khru gang 
[\[17b\]]
[\[17b.1\]]nas brtsams stong phrag brgya/_/rin chen lnga yi phye ma'am/_/yang na 'byung khungs nas 'byung dang /_/sA lu btags pa'i phye ma la/_/snga ma'i rim bzhin 'khor los dbab/_/bden pa'i byin gyi rlabs brjod de/_/gser sogs snod du bdug pa bsreg_/gsal bar bsams 
[\[17b.2\]]nas gsang sngags 'dis/_/mngon par bzlas pa rab tu bya/_/badzra tsi tra sa ma ya hU~M/_rdo rje shugs kyis zhugs nas su/_/chos dbyings kyis ni 'di nyid dag_/sems can dbyings ni rab sgrol mdzad/_/rgyal ba'i mchog ni mi bskyod mgon/_/bde bar gshegs pa kun gyi gnas/_/
[\[17b.3\]]skyon kun las ni grol bar mos/_/bla mas g.yon pa'i khu tshur gyis/_/dbang ldan mtshams nas phyi rol nas/_/ri mo lnga ni bri ba'o/_/dkar po ser po dmar po dang /_/ljang khu nag po nang du'o/_/g.yas su bskor cing chad med pas/_/rdul tshon ri mo bri ba'o/_/sbom por bris na 
[\[17b.4\]]nad 'byung ste/_/phra bas nor zad 'khyog 'thab 'byung /_/chad na slob ma bla ma 'chi/_/de bas phyogs kun mnyam par 'gyur/_/g.yas skor tshul du mi 'debs na/_/tshon la phur bus gdab par bya/_/rdo rje rtse mo gnyis dkar mo/_/gzhan ni ser por shes par bya/_/pad+ma rA ga 'dra ba 
[\[17b.5\]]gzhan/_/ma ra ga ta lta bu ste/_/dbus ma nag por shes byas nas/_/[(]tshom [,]tshon [)]gyi rim pa de ltar ro/_/shar gyi phyogs su dkar po che/_/lho ser nub dmar byang ljang khu/_/dbus ma'i cha phyogs ra bas bskor/_/in+d+ra nI la'i kha dog go/_/de dus rlung dang sbrul sogs sprul/_/bgegs 
[\[17b.6\]]dgra'i phyag rgya dran byas nas/_/sdang ba'i dgra yi sngags rgyal 'di/_/brjod pas de dag sel bar bya/_/na maHsa man+ta kA ya wA ka tsit+ta badzra NAM/_na mo badzra kro d+hA ya/_ma hA daMSh+T+roT ka Ta b+hai ra ba ya a si mu sa la pa ra shu pA sha hastA ya/_oM a mr-i ta kuN+Da li kha kha khA hi khA hi/_tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/_ban+d+ha ban+d+ha/_
[\[17b.7\]]ha na ha na/_da ha da ha/_pa tsa pa tsa/_gardza gardza/_bi [(]spo [,]s+pho [)]Ta ya bi s+pho Ta ya/_sarba big+ha nan bi nA ya ka/_ma hA ga Na pa ti/_dzI ban+ti an+ta ka ra ya hU~M phaT/_de ltar rdul tshon btab gyur nas/_/pad+ma'i gdan la nyi ma dang /_/zla ba rab tu bkod byas nas/_/mtshan ma'i phyag rgya dgod pa'o/_/dbus 
[\[18a\]]
[\[18a.1\]]su rdo rje rtse lnga pa/_/in+d+ra nI la'i kha dog 'dra/_/shar du lce brgyad 'khor lo ni/_/zla ba lta bu'i 'od zer dang /_/lho ru rin chen zur dgu pa/_/margad lta bu'i 'od zer ldan/_/nub tu pad+ma 'dab brgyad pa/_/dmar po 'od zer dang ldan pa/_/byang du ral gri rin chen yu/_/
[\[18a.2\]]sngon po 'od zer dang ldan pa'o/_/me mtshams spyan ni sngon po'o/_/bden bral mtshams su rdo rje bri/_/rlung mtshams pad+ma sdong bur bcas/_/dbang ldan ut+pal ljang ser can/_/phyi rol me mtshams la sogs dang /_/shar sgo g.yas g.yon gru gnyis la/_/me long pi wang dung dang snod/_/gos 
[\[18a.3\]]dang chos kyi 'byung gnas bri/_/tho ba 'bar ba shar sgor bri/_/rdo rje dbyug pa lho sgor bri/_/pad+ma nub kyi sgo ru bri/_/rdo rje kha bye byang du bri/_/shes rab thabs su rab ldan pas/_/rtag tu mnyam par gzhag ste bya/_/rang rang gi ni ngo bo rnams/_/'phro ba'i sngags kyis gdab par bya/_/de nas lha 
[\[18a.4\]]rnams gnas bya'i phyir/_/sme sha can gyi bdag nyid che/_/bu mo lo grangs bcu gnyis pa/_/mA ma kI ru byin gyis brlabs/_/gnyis sbyor rjes su chags bskyed nas/_/sangs rgyas rang gi nang du gzhug_/cho ga gsum gyis bsgrub byas nas/_/bzhi pas gnyis su med par bya/_/rdo rje pad+ma'i sgra 
[\[18a.5\]]yis ni/_/sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor rnam par dgug_/bskrad nas srung bya sngon bzhin du/_/mchod yon la sogs dbul bar bya/_/mchod bstod bdud rtsi myang byas nas/_/shes rab mthar byed kyis gzhug dang /_/bcings te dga' byas nyer sgrub dang /_/sgrub pa dbang bskur gyis 
[\[18a.6\]]mtshan pa'o/_/sngon bzhin mchod pa yang byas te/_/de bzhin bstod dang bdud rtsi myang /_/gsal bar bsgoms nas bzlas brjod dang /_/reg pa'i mchod pas mnyes bya'o/_/de bzhin phyi yi dri la sogs/_/sngags dang phyag rgya dang ldan pas/_/rang gi sa bon las sprul te/_/'khor lo yang 
[\[18a.7\]]dag mchod pa'o/_/'di ni yang dag bdug pa ste/_/rang bzhin dang ni sbyar ba'i dri/_/bdag gis dbul ba 'di bzhes la/_/bdag la bka' drin stsal du gsol/_/badzra d+hUp+pe pU dza s+pha ra Na/_sna tshogs me tog yid 'ong ba/_/rab tu yid 'ong 'bar ba yi/_/byang chub yan lag me tog 'di/_/
[\[18b\]]
[\[18b.1\]]bdag la brtser dgongs bzhes su gsol/_/badzra puSh+pe pU dza s+pha ra Na/_ting 'dzin mar me dge ba 'di/_/sreg byed ngan 'gro 'jigs las skyob/_/lha yi spyan ni thob byed pa/_/bdag la brtser dgongs bzhes su gsol/_/badzra a lo ke pU dza [(]spa [,]s+pha [)]ra Na/_ga pur la sogs byug pa 
[\[18b.2\]]ni/_/dri zhim la sogs me tog rgyan/_/hU~M dbyangs snyan par rol 'byung 'di/_/bdag la brtser dgongs bzhes su gsol/_/badzra gan+d+he pU dza s+pha ra Na/_sna tshogs bza' ba yid 'ong ba/_/bza' dang bcas dang bldag dang gzhib/_/yid 'ong gdung ba sel ba 'di/_/bdag la brtser 
[\[18b.3\]]dgongs bzhes su gsol/_/badzra nai bi t+ye pU dza s+pha ra Na/_sna tshogs btung ba yid 'ong ba/_/'bru yi chang dang bcos pa'i chang /_/myos byed bdud rtsi dpa' bo 'di/_/bdag la brtser dgongs bzhes su gsol/_/badzra su ra pU dza s+pha ra Na/_rin chen ri'am rgya mtsho rin chen rnams/_/bzhad 
[\[18b.4\]]dang sgeg dga' rol dang bde ba dang /_/dpag bsam shing dang na bza' shing rnams kun/_/'khor lo bla mar bcas la dbul bar bya/_/bo d+hi rat+na a laM ka ra/_sa ha la se ra ti kri ha sau khya a nut+ta ra/_a nut+ta ra bo d+h+yaM laM ka ra bas+t+ra pU dza me g+ha sa mu tra s+pha ra Na sa ma ye hU~M zhes dbul lo/_/bsrungs 
[\[18b.5\]]nas phyi rol byung nas kyang /_/phyogs skyong rang rang rnal 'byor gnas/_/sdig med sha khrag chu zan gyi/_/gtor ma phul nas nang du 'gro/_/'di ni gzhan du bshad pas der shes par bya'o/_/gos dang rgyan gyis rab brgyan te/_/rdo rje hU~M mdzad bdag bsgyur la/_/dkyil 'khor bskor 
[\[18b.6\]]ba yang byas te/_/phyag 'tshal me tog sngon po dor/_/bdag pos bcing bsams mgo ru bcings/_/de yi gong 'og cho ga rnams/_/rnam par dag par 'gyur bas na/_/dag pa'i dag pa rnam dag pa'o/_/de nas lha rnams tshim bya'i phyir/_/thab la sogs pa dang ldan pa'i/_/
[\[18b.7\]]sbyin sreg gnas su song nas kyang /_/sbyor ba ldan pas mnyes par bya/_/'di'i cho ga yang gzhan du bshad pas der shes par bya'o/_/dkyil 'khor mchod pa yang byas te/_/shis pa'i phyir na bkra shis glu/_/'khor lo bskor nas bdag nyid kyis/_/pheg rdob pa yi gar gyis 
[\[19a\]]
[\[19a.1\]]kyang /_/hU~M hU~M 'khor ba 'don byed sku 'dzin pa/_/gnyis gnyis 'khyud pa'i sbyor ba'i mchog_/mi bskyod rdo rje khyod 'ba' zhig_/te na hU~M te na hU~M te na te te hU~M/_khyod kyi zhabs kyi pad+ma la/_/lha dang mi yis 'dud pa'i mchog_/sprul pa'i me tog sna tshogs dang /_/mi 
[\[19a.2\]]bskyod rdo rje khyod 'ba' zhig_/te na hU~M te na hU~M te na te te hU~M/_dngos po rnam grol rnam par bsnyen/_/gsang ba'i gsang ba 'dzin pa khyod/_/bdud rtsi mtha' med gsol ba khyod/_/khyod kyi dgongs pa pad rgyas yin/_/te na hU~M te na hU~M te na te te hU~M/_rtsom spangs sems can don mdzad pa/_/rgyu med 
[\[19a.3\]]gzugs la phyag 'tshal lo/_/te na hU~M te na hU~M te na te te hU~M/_dung dang ku mu ta yi gzugs/_/rgya mtsho dngas ma zla gzugs 'dra/_/te na hU~M te na hU~M te na te te hU~M/_rol pa gnyis med rnal 'byor mdzad/_/rdo rje dril bu phyag rgya 'dzin/_/te na hU~M te na hU~M te na te te hU~M/_sgyu 'phrul las kyi 'gro ba bzung /_/rkyen 
[\[19a.4\]]gyis ma byas gzugs can yin/_/te na hU~M te na hU~M te na te te hU~M/_de nas 'og nas 'byung ba'i rim/_/bdag nyid zhugs byas lha yis kyang /_/chu nas bka' stsal bar gyi dbang /_/bskur bsams 'khor lo de la ni/_/slob ma rnams ni gzhug pa'i phyir/_/rdo rje 'chang ba las byung ba'i/_/
[\[19a.5\]]dkyil 'khor rten dang bcas pa yi/_/de bzhin nyid ni lan gsum brjod/_/nam mkha' las byung mtshan ldan phyir/_/thog ma tha ma med pa'i sems/_/rdo rje sems dpa' che rang bzhin/_/mi bskyod rdo rje deng grub shog_/mchog chen dngos grub thams cad dang /_/dbang phyug rdo rje 'dzin 
[\[19a.6\]]pa'i lha/_/thams cad rdo rje 'dzin pa'i gzugs/_/dam pa'i yi ge grub par shog_/skyon med rtag pa la sogs pa/_/'dod chags kun la rjes su chags/_/'dod chags chen po dga' ba che/_/de nyid bcom ldan bdag la grub/_/gtan du dag pa kun gyi mchog_/gdod ma 
[\[19a.7\]]nas grol de bzhin gshegs/_/kun du bzang po kun bdag nyid/_/rdo rje sems dpa' bdag grub bo/_/dngos grub chen po kun gyi mchog_/dbang phyug chen po'i phyag rgya yis/_/rdo rje snying gtso bdag la ni/_/rdo rje sngags chen grub par shog_/sems can kun gyi yid khyab pa/_/
[\[19b\]]
[\[19b.1\]]sems can kun gyi snying la gnas/_/sems can kun gyi de bzhin pha/_/dam tshig mchog gi 'dod pa'i mchog_/bden pa gang gis ye shes kyi/_/shes rab thabs dag dkyil 'khor ni/_/mgon po khyod kyi bden pa des/_/bdag gis 'dod pa yongs su skongs/_/gzugs brnyan 
[\[19b.2\]]dang ni mnyam pa'i chos/_/dag cing dang ba rnyog med pa/_/gzung med brjod du med pa yang /_/rgyu dang rkyen la kun du byung /_/de nyid de nyid bskyed pa ni/_/de dag bden pas dkyil 'khor la/_/gzugs brnyan gsal bar slob pa ni/_/sdig med pas ni blta bar bgyi/_/thun mong sdom 
[\[19b.3\]]pa dang ldan pa/_/bsnyen sgrub gsum gyis bsgrubs nas su/_/yol bas bar du bcad pa la/_/'khyil bzlas chu yis gtor bar bya/_/dmar po sham gyis gdong g.yogs nas/_/dga' ba khyod gang yin zhes dri/_/dga' ba skal ldan bdag yin zhes/_/'jug pa po yis smra 
[\[19b.4\]]bar bya/_/de nas me tog snyim bkang nas/_/'khor los bzhes zhes gtor nas ni/_/gtong sems bskyed nas bla ma la/_/yon ni rab tu dbul bar bya/_/yul mkhar khyim dang glang po rta/_/bran dang bran mo chung ma drug_/bu mo shin tu yid 'ong dang /_/gang gis rang gi lus 'di yang /_/
[\[19b.5\]]deng nas brtsams te dbul par bya/_/ci yi phyir ni dbul bya na/_/slob dpon du ni nyer gnas phyir/_/snying rje chen pos nyer gnas phyir/_/sems can thams cad skyob don du/_/bdag gi lus 'di dbul bar bgyi/_/hU~M gis snying kar rdo rje bsgom/_/oM sarba yo ga tsit+ta ut+pA da ya mi/_
[\[19b.6\]]su ra ta s+t+waM sid+d+hi badzra ya thA su khaM/_brjod pas byang chub sems bskyed la/_/lus kyi 'khor lo bskul bar bya/_/de ring khyod de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis byin gyis rlob par 'gyur ro/_/khyod kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mchog gi gsang chen 'di/_/dkyil 'khor du ma 
[\[19b.7\]]zhugs pa rnams kyi mdun du smra bar mi bya'o/_/dad par mi byed pa yang mi rung ngo /_/zhes brjod par bya'o/_/gshin rje gshed la sogs pa'am/_/a khaM bI ra hU~M brjod nas/_/drangs nas 'jug pa'i sgor gnas te/_/snga ma'i sdom pa brtan par bya/_/de ring khyod de bzhin gshegs pa 
[\[20a\]]
[\[20a.1\]]thams cad kyi rigs la rab tu chud do/_/de bas ngas khyod la rdo rje ye shes bskyed par bya'o/_/ye shes de khyod de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dngos grub kyang thob na/_dngos grub gzhan lta smos kyang ci dgos/_de'i thabs 'di khyod kyis dkyil 'khor ma mthong 
[\[20a.2\]]ba rnams kyi mdun du ma smra shig_/khyod dam tshig las 'da' bar mi rung ba'i phyir ro/_/snying gar rdo rje bzhag nas ni/_/de ring rdo rje sems dpa' nyid/_/khyod kyi snying la yang dag gnas/_/gal te tshul 'di 'das na ni/_/snying 'gas sdug bsngal de ltar 'byung /_/badzra sa twa hU~M/_pad+ma'i ban+d+har 
[\[20a.3\]]bdud rtsi lnga/_/yi ge gsum gyis 'bar byas la/_/rdo rje yis ni de yi khar/_/mi ldog phyir ni blud par bya/_/'di ni khyod kyi dmyal ba'i chu/_/dam las 'da' na sreg par byed/_/dam tshig bsrungs na dngos grub pa'i/_/bdud rtsi rdo rje chu 'thung shig_/badzrod ka Tha/_de nas de yis mi 'gyur byas/_/sems 
[\[20a.4\]]g.yo dogs phyir bstan pa yi/_/dam bcas 'di ni bla ma yis/_/lan gsum du ni bsgrags par bya/_/de ring phyin chad khyod kyis phyag na rdo rje nga yin gyi/_ngas ci smras pa'i bya ba thams cad khyod kyis bya dgos so/_/khyod kyis bdag la brnyas par mi rung ste/_/brnyas na ya nga ba ma spangs pas shi 
[\[20a.5\]]ba'i dus byas nas dmyal bar ltung bas ma 'gro shig_/de nas slob ma de yis kyang /_/brtan nas rang nyid dag bya'i phyir/_/gsol ba gdab pa 'di yi mchog_/smros shig ces ni bla mas smras/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis byin gyis rlobs la rdo rje sems dpa' bdag 
[\[20a.6\]]cag la dbab du gsol lo/_/de yi snying ga ru ni hU~M/_/laM byung dbang chen dbus su bsam/_/mgrin par a ni pa~M byung ba'i/_/chu yi dkyil 'khor dbus su bsam/_/spyi bor ha~M ni dkar po che/_/yaM byung rlung dbus bsams nas ni/_/skul byed dz+haiM ni rkang mthil gnyis/_/rlung gis 
[\[20a.7\]]bskyod pas 'bar byas dbab/_/oM A be+i sha ya staM b+ha ya ra ra ra ra tsa la ya tsa la ya/_hU~M a ha dz+haiM/_rdo rje dril bu sgra dang bcas/_/'jigs rung gar gyis smin ring bskyod/_/mig bgrad so gtsigs bzhad byed pas/_/slob ma dbab pa rab tu bya/_/lce la thig le'i yi ge AH/_/bsams nas 
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]bzlas shing dge mi dge/_bru hi badzra zhes brjod nas/_/dbab pa brtan par bya ba'o/_/pra ti badzra ho brjod la/_/me tog dkyil 'khor dor du gzhug_/de yis bcings par rab bsams la/_/blangs nas mgo ru bcing bar bya/_/'di ni stobs chen sems dpa' bas/_/'di yi ma 
[\[20b.2\]]'dres so sor zung /_/phyag mtshan nye'am mi nye ba/_/re'u cha'i gnas las shes par bya'o/_/yang na rang gi skal ba yis/_/gdong phye me tog de la gtor/_/mig gnyis oM 'bar bsams nas ni/_/gdong phye las kyi kha dog bstan/_/de ring rdo rje sems dpa' nyid/_/nan tan byas 
[\[20b.3\]]te khyod mig phye/_/bla na med pa'i rdo rjes mig_/thams cad la ni mig phye 'gyur/_/dkyil 'khor gtso bo nas brtsams nas/_/bdud rtsi 'khyil par bstan pa'am/_/yang na bdud rtsi 'khyil pa las/_/gtso bo'i bar du bstan bya'o/_/slob mas thal mo sbyar byas la/_/rdo rje dkyil 
[\[20b.4\]]'khor chen po yi/_/dkyil 'khor nyid du bdag zhugs so/_/sbyor ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po yi/_/dkyil 'khor de ring bdag gis mthong /_/gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po yi/_/dkyil 'khor du ni bdag dbang bskur/_/de skad lan gsum brjod par bya/_/bum pa rnams chu 
[\[20b.5\]]gcig tu byas/_/mi bskyod rdo rje rab bskyed la/_/zhugs nas slob ma'i mgo bo ru/_/'khor los sgyur bzhin dbang bskur bya/_/rin chen la sogs dbu rgyan la/_/rin chen 'byung ldan gyur byas te/_/dbus su rang gi lha bzhag pas/_/cod pan dang ldan dbang bskur bya/_/rdo rje rtse mo dgu 
[\[20b.6\]]lnga pa/_/'od dpag med bsgoms yongs gyur te/_/byin nas snying gar de bzung ba/_/rdo rje'i dbang ni bskur bar bya/_/dril bu don yod grub pa ru/_/bsgoms nas yongs gyur byin nas ni/_/rdo rje dril bu la 'khyud tshul/_/bdag por dbang ni bskur bar bya/_/spyi gtsug ltag pa la 
[\[20b.7\]]bcug ste/_/spyi bor phyag reg ming gi dbang /_/che ge rdo rje zhes bos nas/_/khyad par gzhan yang mthong bar bya/_/gang gang snang ba kun bdag ste/_/de yi gtso bo mi bskyod mgon/_/chos rnams rang bzhin gyis dag pas/_/chos kyi dbyings kyi yi ge tshul/_/kun gyi gtso bo yin pa'i 
[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]phyir/_/ming gong dpal gyi sgra gzhug bya/_/go ba nyid du bsgrags pa'i phyir/_/dang po kye sbyor mtha' yi bar/_/hU~M bskyed rdo rje lag byin nas/_/'chang bas rdo rje lan gsum gsor/_/brtul zhugs 'di ni dang po ru/_/rnal 'byor pa yis blang bar bya/_/
[\[21a.2\]]'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad dang /_/rdo rje sems dpa'i phyag na gnas/_/rtag par khyod kyis kyang bzung ste/_/lag na rdo rje brtul zhugs brtan/_/oM sarba ta thA ga ta sid+d+hi badzra sa ma ya tiSh+Tha e Sha twaM d+ha ra ya mi/_hi hi hi hi hU~M/_de yi de nyid bshad par bya/_/sngon mtha' med pa'i 
[\[21a.3\]]sems dpa' ni/_/rdo rje sems dpa' dga' ba che/_/kun du bzang po kun gyi bdag_/rdo rje snying po'i gtso bo'i gtso/_/a las byung ba'i dril bu ni/_/lag g.yon byin nas phyogs brgyad dkrol/_/brtul zhugs 'di yang dang po ru/_/de yi grogs su sbyin pa'o/_/'di ni 
[\[21a.4\]]sangs rgyas thams cad kyi/_/shes rab dbyangs chen 'khrol bar 'dod/_/rtag par khyod kyis kyang bzung ste/_/rgyal ba byang chub mchog tu 'dod/_/de nyid bshad par bya ba ni/_/'di ni khyod kyis dkrol bya ste/_/srid pa rang bzhin gyis dag phyir/_/rang bzhin gyis ni 'byung bar byed/_/
[\[21a.5\]]rang bzhin dag pas sems can kun/_/dam pa'i srid pa byed pa po/_/slob ma rdo rje dbyings phyug dang /_/ldan par bsams nas bcom ldan 'das/_/mngon byed 'das pa mchog 'di yis/_/phyag rgya'i dam tshig sbyin pa'o/_/phyag rgya chen po'i byin brlabs te/_/snying po bsnyen pa la sogs 
[\[21a.6\]]grags/_/dam tshig 'dod gzugs sogs rnams kyis/_/sngags ni dbugs su rtogs nas bzla/_/rang gi rang bzhin de rnams kyis/_/phyogs bcu dus gsum khyab pa yi/_/ma lus gtso bo mchod byas nas/_/bdag dang gzhan yang mnyes par bya/_/dka' thub dam bcas de dag 
[\[21a.7\]]gis/_/bstan par byas kyang 'grub pa med/_/mi snang mngon shes mkha' 'gro med/_/sku gsung thugs las grub skyes so/_/de bas sangs rgyas sems can rnams/_/sngags kyi spyod pa mchog bzung ste/_/'dod pa kun la longs spyod pas/_/chos kyi yi ge mchog thob 'gyur/_/
[\[21b\]]
[\[21b.1\]]bde sdug gnyis med bdag nyid kyis/_/'dod pa'i yon tan lnga bsten te/_/chags chen ye shes don sbyor na/_/de kho na nyid kun kyang 'grub/_/sku gsung thugs grub yang dag ste/_/gang gzhan dman pa yang skye 'dod/_/de yang sku gsung thugs bsgoms nas/_/sngags bzlas 
[\[21b.2\]]pa yis 'grub pa'o/_/thun mong ma yin sdom par ldan/_/sbyor ba ma nyams yon phul nas/_/rdo rje slob dpon dbang nod phyir/_/de yis lan gsum gsol ba gdab/_/byang chub rdo rje sangs rgyas la/_/mchod chen ji ltar bgyis pa bzhin/_/bdag kyang rab tu bsgral ba'i phyir/_/
[\[21b.3\]]nam mkha' rdo rje bdag la stsol/_/'jug pa'i sgo ru khru do bar/_/pad+ma dkar po 'dab brgyad la/_/sa bon gyis mtshan dbang stegs bri/_/seng ge mtshan par 'dug tu gzhug_/de nyid ting 'dzin gsum ldan par/_/byas nas bla ma'i lus dkyil 'khor/_/sangs rgyas sogs spro 
[\[21b.4\]]rnam dgug cing /_/nam mkha'i khams kun dga' bar bya/_/de yis rnga dril sil [(]snyen [,]snyan [)]dang /_/rgyal mtshan [(]'phen [,]'phan [)]sogs pheg rdob cing /_/bum pa gtso sogs gzung byas nas/_/sngon bzhin chu sogs dbang bskur bsam/_/dbang bskur rdo rje chen po ni/_/khams gsum kun gyis 
[\[21b.5\]]phyag byas pa/_/gsang gsum gnas las byung ba yi/_/sangs rgyas kun gyis sbyin pa yin/_/de skad lan gsum brjod nas su/_/dge ba'i phyir dang shis pa'i phyir/_/bum pa spyi bor rab bzung ste/_/bkra shis dbyangs 'di brjod par bya/_/dpal ldan gser gyi ri bo ltar 'bar ba/_/
[\[21b.6\]]'jig rten gsum mgon dri ma gsum dang bral/_/bcom ldan pad rgyas lta bu'i spyan mnga' ba/_/bkra shis des ni deng khyod zhi byed shog_/de yis bstan pa'i dri med mchog rab ni/_/'jig rten gsum grags lha dang mis mchod pa/_/cho ga skye dgu rnams ni zhi bar byed/_/bkra shis gnyis 
[\[21b.7\]]pa deng 'dir khyed la shog_/dam pa'i chos ldan thos gang bkra shis shing /_/dge 'dun lha dang lha mi'i mchod 'os pa/_/ngo mtshar dpal rab mchog rab tshogs rnams kyi/_/bkra shis gsum pa deng 'dir khyed la shog_/de nas de dag thams cad kun/_/ye shes ngo bor zhugs bsams 
[\[22a\]]
[\[22a.1\]]pas/_/dbu rgyan ye shes sems dpa' bskyed/_/bskor byas slob dpon gnas par bya/_/sngon bzhin mchod dang bstod pa dang /_/bdud rtsi myang ba yang byas nas/_/dkyil 'khor de bzhin nyid bstan bya/_/sangs rgyas ma rgyas mnyam pa'i phyir/_/gru bzhi 
[\[22a.2\]]ru ni rab tu bstan/_/dran pa nyer bzhag shar gyi sgo/_/yang dag spong ba lho yi sgo/_/rdzu 'phrul rkang pa nub kyi sgo/_/dbang po stobs ni byang gi sgo/_/bsam gtan bzhi ni twa ra Na/_/ting 'dzin rnams ni kha khyer brgyad/_/gzungs rnams der gnas lha mo'o/_/bsam pa bskang phyir 
[\[22a.3\]]lha tshogs rgyan/_/gsung rab yan lag 'phags sogs so/_/byang chub yan lag dra ba sogs/_/rnam par thar brgyad ka ba brgyad/_/sngags tshul rdo rje phreng ba zlum/_/phyi logs rnam dag yon tan lnga/_/dri med stobs sogs mchod pa lnga/_/bum pa chus gang thugs rje dang /_/
[\[22a.4\]]tshogs rdzogs ser po ye shes gter/_/'gro ba rnams dang dbyer med kyang /_/de yi nyes pas mi gos phyir/_/sna tshogs pad+ma bcu dgu ni/_/sdong bu dang ni bcas pa'o/_/mi dge 'dam rdzab skam mdzad phyir/_/dge ba'i myu gu gso mdzad pa/_/nyi ma'i gdan ni bdun rnams so/_/nyon 
[\[22a.5\]]mongs tsha ba bsil byed dang /_/ma rig mun nag sel ba'i phyir/_/zla ba bcu gnyis dang ldan pa'o/_/tshor ba gsob la sogs pa dang /_/'ju ba la sogs dus bcu gnyis/_/zad dus sgron bdun rnam dag pas/_/grangs ni nges par bstan pa'o/_/nyon mongs thams cad las grol 
[\[22a.6\]]phyir/_/gzhal yas khang du btags pa yin/_/snying po la ni 'dus pa'i phyir/_/dkyil 'khor zhes su bshad pa yin/_/rnam par shes pa zhe sdang thugs/_/chos dbyings rnam dag mi bskyod pa/_/gzugs phung gti mug che dang sku/_/me long rnam dag rnam snang mdzad/_/tshor ba'i 
[\[22a.7\]]phung po mnyam pa nyid/_/nga rgyal rnam dag rin chen 'byung /_/'du shes phung po 'dod chags gsum/_/so sor rtog pa snang mtha' yas/_/'du byed phung po nan tan dang /_/phrag dog rnam dag don yod grub/_/sa dang stong nyid thabs dang ni/_/snying rje zhi byed spyan ma'o/_/byams dang mtshan med 
[\[22b\]]
[\[22b.1\]]smon lam dang /_/chu khams srung byed mA ma kI/_/dga' dang smon med stobs dang ni/_/me khams rgyas byed na bza' dkar/_/btang snyoms ye shes rang bzhin gsal/_/rlung khams sgrol byed sgrol ma'o/_/sbyin pa rnam dag gzugs rdo rje/_/tshul khrims rnam dag sgra rdo rje/_/bzod 
[\[22b.2\]]pa rnam dag dri rdo rje/_/brtson 'grus rnam dag ro rdo rje/_/bsam gtan rnam dag reg rdo rje/_/shes rab rnam dag chos rdo rje/_/phyi yi longs spyod kyis mchod phyir/_/dbyung sngags ting 'dzin gsungs pa'o/_/mi la sogs pa rnam dag phyir/_/de nyid du ni bkod par gsungs/_/dbyung 
[\[22b.3\]]sngags ting 'dzin ma gsungs pas/_/sa snying la sogs phyir mi dgod/_/'jigs sogs shes bya phung po'i bdud/_/dad dag gshin rje gshed 'gugs pa/_/nyon mongs de bdud mthar 'dzin lha/_/brtson dag 'jug byed shes mthar byed/_/skye sgrib 'chi bdag log par lta/_/
[\[22b.4\]]dran dag 'ching byed rta mgrin no/_/las dang lha bdud mchog 'dzin lha/_/ting 'dzin mgu mdzad bdud rtsi 'khyil/_/chos dang longs spyod sprul pa dang /_/ngo bo nyid de rnam bzhi ni/_/thugs sogs rdo rje ye shes sems/_/phyag rgya bzhi zhes bshad pa yin/_/rnam par rtog pa dang bral 
[\[22b.5\]]phyir/_/gting dpag dka' bas zab mo ste/_/phyag rgya chen por rab snang ba/_/rang gzhan smin byed gsal ba po/_/zab mo dang ni yang dag gsal/_/shes rab thabs kyi bdag nyid de/_/mtha' gnyis skyon ni rab bkag pas/_/dus thams cad du dbyer med do/_/de bzhin slob 
[\[22b.6\]]ma dbang bskur dang /_/dngos grub sgrub pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi/_/sgrub pa'i thabs ni rab bstan pas/_/gang zag gnyis su shes par byed/_/gzugs brnyan glegs bam la sogs pa/_/yod na khyod kyis rab gnas mdzod/_/de ni ji srid bar du ni/_/sems can don du 'gyur 
[\[22b.7\]]ba'o/_/de ltar lung bstan dbugs dbyung dang /_/bka' stsal pa yis dbang bskur nas/_/bskyed pa'i rim pa kun bstan te/_/bka' lung g.yar gyi blang ba'o/_/bum pa'i dbang gi cho ga'o/_/lo 'am yang na zla ba ru/_/rig dang slob dpon dbang thob pas/_/lus dang 
[\[23a\]]
[\[23a.1\]]zang zing thabs dang bcas/_/spyod pa mchog gis bla ma bkur/_/rin chen sna bdun la sogs pa/_/rjes su mthun pa dbul bya ste/_/bla ma dgyes par byed pa'i ched/_/bu mo gzhon nu gzugs bzang ba/_/rgyan ldan dam sdom dbang bcas pa/_/lus rgyas 
[\[23a.2\]]mdzes pa dbul byas nas/_/bla ma'i g.yon du bu mo bzhag_/rgyal po mu stegs chom rkun gyis/_/gnod med dben pa'i sa phyogs su/_/mchog gi dris byugs pad sogs gdan/_/bla re rgyal mtshan sogs kyis bklubs/_/rol mo sil snyan sna tshogs sgras/_/gong gi 
[\[23a.3\]]'khor ram yang na sku/_/tshogs kyi mchod pa chen po yis/_/dbyer med 'khor lo mchod byas nas/_/me tog snyim bkang gsol ba gdab/_/kun mkhyen ye shes phung po sogs/_/pus btsugs thal sbyar mgrin dma' bas/_/gsang ba mchog gi sdom pa che/_/yang dag 
[\[23a.4\]]blangs nas bsrung bar bya/_/ji ltar dbang bskur sogs tshul gyis/_/sangs rgyas la sogs thob pa bzhin/_/byang chub spyod pa gzhan gyis ni/_/'dul ba'i dge ba de bzhin med/_/de bas dam tshig bus srungs shig_/rtag tu sdom pa yang skyongs shig_/sha lnga bdud rtsi lnga 
[\[23a.5\]]bza' ste/_/de las gzhan yang dam tshig srungs/_/dkon mchog bla ma brnyas byed gzhom/_/gzhan gyi bud med spyad pa dang /_/ma byin par yang blangs byas nas/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]gyi tshig kyang smra bar bya/_/rnal 'byor rgyud las gsungs pa bzhin/_/de ni cho ga bzhin du srungs/_/de ltar bya'o 
[\[23a.6\]]zhes gsol nas/_/yang na me tog phreng ba gtor/_/thal mo sbyar nas gsang ba yi/_/dbang nod phyir ni gsol ba gdab/_/rdo rje sems khyod la sogs pa/_/tshigs bcad lnga yis lan gsum du/_/zhugs nas slob ma dam pa ni/_/mdzes pa'i chas ldan gyis bya'o/_/de nas bla 
[\[23a.7\]]ma yon tan mtsho/_/mchog gi mchog gis de gsan nas/_/zhal ni mdangs dang ldan byas nas/_/snying rje de la bskyed par bya/_/gzugs sogs phun sum tshogs ldan pa'i/_/lha mo de ni byin brlabs nas/_/thub pa'i dbang po zhugs byas nas/_/rdo rje nang du gnas par bya/_/mthe 
[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]bo srin lag gis bkab nas/_/slob ma dpal ldan snang mdzad khar/_/spro ba'i sngags kyis phab nas su/_/gnyi gas 'di ni brjod par bya/_/a ho a ho su kha ho/_/gzhan yang blo yi bye brag gis/_/dbang bskur byas nas mgrin pa ru/_/de nyid myong bar byas nas su/_/de 
[\[23b.2\]]nas 'di yis bsngags pa'o/_/de 'di rdo rje thams cad kyi/_/dbang bskur mchog tu bsngags pa ste/_/thams cad sngags ni 'grub 'gyur te/_/las mchog khyad par can yang 'grub/_/zla ba za ba rab byung ba/_/lung bstan dbugs dbyung bka' stsal pa/_/'og nas 'byung 
[\[23b.3\]]bzhin sbyin par bya/_/rdo rje bzlas pas phyi nang tshogs/_/sgyu ma smig rgyu la sogs pa'i/_/'dod don phyag mtshan mdog dang dbyibs/_/bsam mi khyab pa'i rnam smin sku/_/bsgom pa la gnas de bsgom gzhug_/gsang ba'i dbang gi cho ga'o/_/de bzhin gong ma'i rim pa yis/_/
[\[23b.4\]]mtshan dang ldan pa'i rig pa che/_/btsal te bla ma dag la yon/_/rjes mthun dang bcas dbul bar bya/_/sngon bzhin gsol btab sdom pa blangs/_/sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang /_/nyan thos rang rgyal de bzhin te/_/sku gsung thugs su sbyor ba na/_/phyag 'tshal na ni ma rung 
[\[23b.5\]]'gyur/_/kun du rdo rje sems dpa' yi/_/lus ngag yid kyi dkyil 'khor la/_/bsam gtan rdo rje gsum sbyor bas/_/sngags zlos pas ni bsam gtan bya/_/mdor na sems can thams cad ni/_/bsam gtan rdo rje bskul bar bya/_/de 'di rdo rje thams cad kyi/_/dam chos rab tu 
[\[23b.6\]]bstan pa'o/_/dkyil 'khor dag kyang yang mi bri/_/glegs bam bklag par mi bya zhing /_/mchod rten dag gi las mi bya/_/rdo rje gsum mchog phyag mi bya/_/gal te byang chub mchog 'dod na/_/lag pa'i phyag rgya mi bcing ngo /_/de ltar bya zhes gsol bar bya/_/yang ni me tog gtor 
[\[23b.7\]]byas nas/_/mchod sbyin rdo rje la sogs pa'i/_/tshigs bcad lan gsum gsol btab nas/_/shes rab ye shes yang dag blang /_/de nas lha mo bdag nyid dang /_/dam tshig sdom pa'i mchog smras nas/_/bde chen za ma tog gi ni/_/lha mo'i bsngags pa rab tu brjod/_/de dag 
[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]mdun du gnas pa'i lag_/g.yas g.yon 'og dang steng bzhag nas/_/byin gyis brlabs pa'i lha mo 'di/_/sngags 'di yis ni sbyin par bya/_/yid 'ong gzugs ma 'di nyid ni/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi spyod du gnang /_/dkyil 'khor 'khor lo rim pa yis/_/
[\[24a.2\]]dam pa'i bde ba khyod kyis myong /_/de nas rab mnyam bzhag pa yis/_/dal ba'i bya ba rnam gsum gyis/_/rdo rje skyil krung nang du sems/_/rin chen dkyil du rtogs pa bya/_/rang gi lha yi 'khor lor ldan/_/dbyings kyi dbang phyug dang 'brel pa/_/yol bas bcad de bde myong la/_/
[\[24a.3\]]sngon bzhin dbang bskur byas nas thim/_/oM sarba ta thA ga ta a nu rA ga badzra swa b+hA ba At+ma ko\u0f85 haM/_gti mug rang bzhin gti mug sbyor/_/gti mug dga' dang yang dag ldan/_/gti mug rgyun gyis dbang bskur na/_/gti mug rdo rje'i bdag nyid 'gyur/_/zhe sdang rang bzhin zhe sdang sbyor/_/
[\[24a.4\]]zhe sdang dga' ma dang ldan par/_/zhe sdang rgyun gyis dbang bskur na/_/zhe sdang rdo rje'i bdag nyid 'gyur/_/'dod chags rang bzhin 'dod chags sbyor/_/'dod chags dga' dang yang dag ldan/_/'dod chags rgyun gyis dbang bskur na/_/'dod chags rdo rje'i bdag nyid 'gyur/_/
[\[24a.5\]]shes rab ye shes bdag sbyar na/_/rdo rje dga' ma dang ldan par/_/ye shes rgyun gyis dbang bskur na/_/shes rab ye shes bdag nyid 'gyur/_/shes rab ye shes so/_/de nas bla mas g.yon pa yi/_/phyag ni slob ma'i spyi bor bzhag_/de bzhin gshegs kun dpang byas la/_/
[\[24a.6\]]rig pa'i brtul zhugs sbyin pa'o/_/thabs gzhan gyis ni sangs mi rgya/_/de bas rig ma 'di ni mchog_/chos kun gnyis su med pa la/_/gnyis kyis rnam par mtshon pa yin/_/de bas rtag tu 'khor ba ru/_/khyed kyis 'bral bar ma byed cig_/'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi/_/
[\[24a.7\]]rig pa'i brtul zhugs bla na med/_/blun chen gang zhig 'da' byed na/_/mchog gi dngos grub de la med/_/de ltar de bzhin gzhir yang grags/_/sngon bzhin gnyis ni rgyas btab pas/_/gsal zhing rab dga' mkha' 'dra ba'i/_/man ngag tu ni de nyid brtag_/bla ma de bzhin 
[\[24b\]]
[\[24b.1\]]gshegs gyur na/_/snying gar khu tshur chos gos g.yon/_/g.yas pas mchog sbyin phyag rgya bya/_/bdag nyid bzhengs nas lung ston bya/_/oM khyod la bdag gis lung 'di ston/_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/srid pa'i ngan 'gro thar byas nas/_/yang dag srid pa 
[\[24b.2\]]grub pa'i phyir/_/sa bla sa steng sa 'og tu/_/kye rdo rje sems dpa' zhes bya ba/_/rang byung rang nyid grub gyur cig_/sngags pa gang la lung 'di bstan/_/de ni 'gro ba kun gyi gtso/_/bla na med pa'i byang chub la/_/bde gshegs kun gyis lung bstan yin/_/lung bstan pa'o/_/ji 
[\[24b.3\]]ltar dbang bskur thob pa yis/_/slob mas yon tan brjod par mos/_/dam tshig gsum gyis dbugs phyung nas/_/'di yis bsngags pa brjod pa'o/_/dam tshig gsang chen dkyil 'khor la/_/rab tu zhugs nas mthong bas na/_/khyod ni sdig pa kun las grol/_/de ring shin tu 
[\[24b.4\]]kho nar gnas/_/bde chen theg pa 'di la ni/_/phyin chad shi ba yong med de/_/mi thub mi shigs gzhan gyis kyang /_/rol zhing 'jigs par ga la 'gyur/_/sdug bsngal srid las mya ngan 'das/_/srid pa gtan du dag pa'i phyir/_/yang dag 'byung ba ston pa'i mchog_/srid pa 
[\[24b.5\]]gtan du dngos grub po/_/dngos grub dam tshig sdom pa 'di/_/rig ma rtag tu bsrung bar bya/_/dam pa'i shes rab bstan pa ni/_/sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam par gsungs/_/byang chub sems ni spang mi bya/_/gang zhig rdo rje phyag rgya zhes/_/de ni bskyed pa tsam gyis kyang /_/sangs rgyas 
[\[24b.6\]]nyid du the tshom med/_/dam pa'i chos ni smad mi bya/_/ma rig pa dang gti mug gis/_/de ni bor bar mi bya'o/_/rang bdag yongs su spangs pa dang /_/dka' thub sdug bsngal gzir mi bya/_/ji ltar bde ba 'di bzung nas/_/rdzogs sangs rgyas pa 'di las 'byung /_/rdo rje dril bu'i phyag 
[\[24b.7\]]rgya rnams/_/nam yang spang bar byar mi rung /_/bla ma sangs rgyas dang mnyam phyir/_/rdo rje slob dpon smad mi bya/_/de ni sangs rgyas kun mnyam phyir/_/slob dpon la ni smod byed na/_/sangs rgyas thams cad smad pa yin/_/des ni rtag tu sdug bsngal thob/_/rims dang 'or dang dug dang nad/_/mkha' 
[\[25a\]]
[\[25a.1\]]'gros gtses dang bzung ba dang /_/bgegs dang tshogs bdag drag po yis/_/bsad nas dmyal bar 'gro bar 'gyur/_/de bas nan tan thams cad kyis/_/mchog gi dge ba mngon 'gyur ba'i/_/rdo rje slob dpon bla chen la/_/nam yang brnyas par byar mi rung /_/
[\[25a.2\]]gus pas bla ma yon du ni/_/rjes su 'thad pas dbul bar bya/_/des ni rims nad la sogs pa/_/phyi nas 'byung bar mi 'gyur ro/_/rtag tu rang gi dam tshig bsgrub/_/rtag tu de bzhin gshegs pa mchod/_/rtag tu bla ma la yang dbul/_/sangs rgyas kun dang de mnyam phyir/_/de byin 
[\[25a.3\]]sangs rgyas thams cad la/_/rtag tu byin pa nyid du 'gyur/_/de byin bsod nams tshogs yin pas/_/tshogs la byang chub mchog tu 'gyur/_/ci phyir shin tu 'dir gnas pas/_/khyed kyi tshe 'di 'bras bur bcas/_/dam tshig lha dang mnyam pa ru/_/gnas par the tshom med pa'o/_/rdo rje 
[\[25a.4\]]dang bcas sangs rgyas kyis/_/de ring khyed rnams dbang bskur bas/_/rgyal po chen po khams gsum na/_/rgyal po gtso bo brtan par 'gyur/_/de ring bdud rnams rnam bcom nas/_/gzhal yas khang mchog du chud phyir/_/sangs rgyas nyid phyir de kho nar/_/khyab bdag gis ni 
[\[25a.5\]]the tshom med/_/rab tu dang ba'i yid kyis ni/_/rdo rje de dag kun mdzod cig_/zad med rang gi dam tshig bde/_/bde bar sems bskyed la bsnyen cing /_/myur bar 'gro ba'i bde chu ru/_/rdo rje sems dpa' 'dra ba yi/_/mnyam pa de ru gyur nas su/_/khyod rnams de la 'gro bar shog_/
[\[25a.6\]]dbugs dbyung ba'o/_/de nas slob mas phyag 'tshal te/_/bla ma'i zhabs kyi bka' drin gyis/_/sogs pa'i tshig gis thams cad kun/_/gnang ba'i dbang ni blang bar bya/_/ji lta ji ltar 'dul 'gyur ba/_/sems can rang bzhin du 'gro bgyis/_/de lta de ltar sems can don/_/'dod 
[\[25a.7\]]chags la sogs dag par bya/_/nyin re yi ni mtshams gsum la/_/ting 'dzin gsum gyi sbyor ldan ni/_/bsgrubs nas sgrub pa thun mong dang /_/cig shos dngos grub bsgrub par bya/_/mi snang stong ni chung ngu la/_/mngon shes nyi stong bar ma la/_/rig 'dzin dag ni stong gsum 
[\[25b\]]
[\[25b.1\]]la/_/rdo rje 'dzin pa 'gro kun gtso/_/zhi ba rgyas pa sogs las gang /_/de las gzhan nam gang 'dod la/_/dkyil 'khor kun du chags sbyor na/_/bsgrub pa myur du 'grub par 'ong /_/rdo rje bdag po thams cad kyis/_/'dod chags de nyid don bsams te/_/sems can thams 
[\[25b.2\]]cad phan byed pa'i/_/byang chub 'dod chags las skyes mdzad/_/de bas byang chub 'dod bsngags pas/_/sku gsung thugs ni bsgom byas te/_/cho ga bzhin du las byed na/_/de kun rtogs par 'gyur ba yin/_/de dag byang chub thob 'dod kun/_/dga' ba'i sems kyis 
[\[25b.3\]]thams cad dbul/_/gang zhig phongs pas yid mi bde/_/dad pa'i mchog ldan de yin no/_/bka' stsal pa'i dbang ngo /_/dad pa mchog tu gus ldan pas/_/bla ma'i zhabs ni spyi bor blang /_/ji ltar rje btsun bka' stsal ltar/_/de ltar bya zhes spyan sngar gsol/_/bkra 
[\[25b.4\]]shis smon lam dang ldan par/_/yang dag gnas par gyur cig brjod/_/snod rung byas pa'i slob ma la/_/bla ma shin tu dgyes pa na/_/de dag kun las gsang ba dang /_/rab gsang shin tu gsang ba yi/_/bsgrags min mna' yis btab byas nas/_/som nyi med par bshad 
[\[25b.5\]]par bya/_/'byor ldan slob ma kun tshogs nas/_/sbyin sreg gnas su gnas byas nas/_/bla ma slob ma sbyor bar ldan/_/mar la sogs pa rgya chen rnams/_/slob mas g.yon pa'i mthe bong ni/_/bla ma'i g.yas pa'i mthe bong dang /_/gung mo yis ni gzung byas nas/_/sdig pa 
[\[25b.6\]]rnams ni zhi bar bya/_/de bzhin de ru sa bdag dang /_/phyogs skyong rgyal po la sogs pa/_/zhi bar bya phyir bdun nam ni/_/yang na gsum mam gcig tu bya'o/_/bdag nyid bshad dang nyan pa dang /_/bsam dang bsgom dang dngos grub bsgrub/_/tha na 'jig rten las grub dang /_/khyod kyis 
[\[25b.7\]]grogs ni mdzod cig ces/_/gsol ba btab nas gshegs su gsol/_/sngon bzhin mchod pa thams cad dang /_/glu gar sil snyan la sogs dang /_/bza' dang btung ba'i mchog rnams kyis/_/bdag gzhan 'khor lo mnyes par bya/_/de nas pog snod lag thogs te/_/
[\[26a\]]
[\[26a.1\]]dril bu gsil nas pus mo btsugs/_/ye shes 'khor lo thams cad kun/_/sngags 'dis gshegs su gsol ba'o/_/oM sems can kun don mdzad nas su/_/dngos grub stsal te rigs pa bzhin/_/sangs rgyas yul du gshegs su gsol/_/
[\[26a.2\]]yang ni 'byon par mdzod cig muH/_/de bzhin oM dang AHdang hU~M/_/brjod pas ting 'dzin 'khor lo rnams/_/rdo rje gsum la bsdu byas nas/_/de yang nam mkha' lta bur mos/_/oM a kA ro mu khaM sarba d+harmA NAM/_Ad+ya nu t+pa n+na twAta oM AHhU~M swA hA zhes brjod pas/_gzugs brnyan 
[\[26a.3\]]stong pa nyid dmigs la/_/snod gtsang ma ru bsdus nas su/_/'dod pa la ni nyung shas sbyin/_/lhag ma chu yi skye bo sogs/_/phan phyir gdugs dang rgyal mtshan dang /_/'phan sogs sil snyan rab ldan pa/_/dge 'dun tshogs kyis bteg nas su/_/chu bo chen por blug par bya/_/
[\[26a.4\]]rdo rje rtse mos ri mo dbyi/_/bum pa bla re la sogs pa/_/spyad rung bla mas blang bya ste/_/lhag ma gong bzhin dor bar bya/_/dkyil 'khor 'di ru gzugs brnyan dang /_/glegs bam la sogs rab gnas kyi/_/cho ga bya ba gzhan du bshad/_/mkhas pa rnams kyi der shes bya/_/'di ni 
[\[26a.5\]]dang po rnal 'byor pa'i/_/dkyil 'khor cho ga dang por gsungs/_/gzhan du khyad par ma gtogs pa/_/cho ga rgyas par 'dir shes bya/_/cung zad ye shes dbang thob pas/_/'bebs pa'i cho gas bri bar gsungs/_/khams gsum skye bos smon 'dzugs phyir/_/rnam pa gzhan du 
[\[26a.6\]]mi bya'o/_/ri rtse nyams ni dga' ba dang /_/nags tshal rab tu dben pa'am/_/mchod rten kun dga' ra ba sogs/_/dgon pa'i gnas su spyad bya'o/_/bu mo lo grangs bcu gnyis ma/_/de bzhin bcu gnyis lon pa'i khye'u/_/cho rigs btsun zhing drang por skyes/_/rma sogs spangs 
[\[26a.7\]]dang bzhin mdzes shing /_/so yi thags bzang rab gtsang ba/_/skra gnag mig dkyus ring ba dang /_/rang bzhin dul ba rig pa che/_/bzung nas dbab par rab tu brtag_/de la gong du smos pa dang /_/snod rung sogs kyis zhus nas su/_/de rnams don du tshogs skyed phyir/_/rnam par 
[\[26b\]]
[\[26b.1\]]dag pa'i bzlas pa bya/_/de bzhin sa btsal la sogs nas/_/sbran pa'i cho ga'i bar byas nas/_/lha dang bum pa sems can rnams/_/sta gon du ni gnas par bya/_/thig skud rdul tshon byin brlabs na/_/gong gsungs snod ni khrus byas te/_/gos dang rgyan gyis rab bklubs te/_/ri ska 
[\[26b.2\]]la sogs kyis bdug bya/_/de yi gnas gsum snga ma bzhin/_/dbang chen chu dang rlung dkyil 'khor/_/yi ge gsum gyi byin brlabs la/_/rkang mthil gnyis su skul byed pa/_/rlung gis sbar bas sdig pa bsreg_/ye shes yang dag dbab pa'i phyir/_/rigs gsum bdag 
[\[26b.3\]]nyid rdo rje dril/_/'jigs rung gar dang bcas pa yis/_/de yi snying ga phye byas nas/_/chu dang mar bzhin ro gcig gyur/_/gzi byin la sogs ldan gyur na/_/babs pa'i rtags kyis shes par bya/_/spyan dang rnam par snang mdzad zhugs/_/khru gang tsam mam khru do 'am/_/khru lnga 
[\[26b.4\]]tsam mam brgyad tsam du/_/'jigs shing skrag tshul 'bar bar 'gyur/_/snang ba mtha' yas gos dkar zhugs/_/phreng ba'i sngags dang dbyung ba'i sngags/_/de bzhin snying po rig pa sogs/_/ma bslabs par yang brjod par 'gyur/_/mi bskyod mA ma kI babs pas/_/'das 
[\[26b.5\]]dang ma 'ongs dus dag gi/_/ji ltar byung 'byung dngos po rnams/_/skad cig gis ni yang dag ston/_/de nas sngags kyis bstan par bya/_/de gnyis bla ma slob ma'i tshul/_/thig 'debs dang ni tshon 'gyed dang /_/phyag rgya'am sku sogs 'god par byed/_/de nas mdun du 'dug byas 
[\[26b.6\]]nas/_/ji ltar bgyi zhes bka' nod byed/_/mchod pa sna tshogs rab byas la/_/ye shes lha ni gshegs su gsol/_/snod gnyis zas dang skom rnams dang /_/yon gyis mnyes par byas nas su/_/grangs lhag rung bar bya ba ste/_/rang gi gnas su gtang bya'o/_/de nas 'khor lo 
[\[26b.7\]]gnas bsam dang /_/mchod dang zhugs dang dbang bskur ba/_/bsdu ba dang po rnal 'byor bzhin/_/cho ga rgyas par bya ba'o/_/'di'am gong du slob ma'am/_/gzhan don rab tu bya ba'i phyir/_/bri ba'i gsung gi cho ga ni/_/gzhan du bshad pas der shes bya/_/'di ni cung zad dbang gyur 
[\[27a\]]
[\[27a.1\]]pa'i/_/bri ba'i cho ga gsungs pa'o/_/sgrub pa la yang ri mo ni/_/tshul 'di dang ni mtshungs zhes bya/_/ye shes dbang sgyur rnal 'byor pas/_/sgyu ma lta bu'i ting 'dzin gyis/_/dkyil 'khor rgyal mchog las rgyal mchog_/rdzogs pa'i bsam gtan 
[\[27a.2\]]gyis bsgrub bya/_/bla ma'i man ngag rab thob cing /_/sbyor shes de dag lhur len pa/_/rtags shes dang po pha rol phyin/_/yul la zhen pa dang bral ba'i/_/bdag nyid chen po nyag gcig des/_/de 'dra'i sa bcu mgon po che/_/rang gi nus pa dang phrad pa/_/mnyes bya de yis yang dag 
[\[27a.3\]]blang /_/me tog la sogs mchod pa'i tshogs/_/phyi rol nang du bcas sngon song /_/thal sbyar pus mo btsugs nas ni/_/bla chen de la gsol ba btab/_/'jig rten sgron mchog rnal 'byor kun la 'phags/_/khyod ni skyon rnams dag pa'i pha rol phyin/_/srid pa'i mtsho las 
[\[27a.4\]]sems can kun sgrol ba/_/ded dpon chen po khyed la skyabs su mchi/_/bdag cag thabs bral 'khor bar 'khyams gyur te/_/ma rig mun pas bsgribs pas dmus long gyur/_/sdug bsngal gyis gzir yang dag don ma rnyed/_/bdag nyid chen po khyod kyis bskyab tu gsol/_/
[\[27a.5\]]de skad gsol nas de yi tshe/_/bla ma bdag nyid chen po des/_/snying rje chen po rab bskyed nas/_/tshig 'dis gnang ba sbyin pa'o/_/bu tshur shog cig bsam gtan gyi/_/'khor lo chen po nga yis bstan/_/khyod kyis kyang ni mnyam zhog la/_/nga yi bkod pa lta bar gyis/_/de skad 
[\[27a.6\]]gsol nas bsam gtan gyi/_/dkyil 'khor rim pa 'di yis bsgom/_/lhun gyis grub pa'i sbyor ba ni/_/skad cig ye shes kyis rdzogs pa'o/_/sangs rgyas 'khor lo sbyar ba yis/_/bsam gtan 'khor lo rab rdzogs bya/_/ye shes 'khor lo bsam gtan gyis/_/'khor 
[\[27a.7\]]lo can kun mngon sum zhugs/_/gong gsungs mchod pa'i cho ga ni/_/bsam gtan ldan pas mnyes par bya/_/de nyid brjod pas 'khor lo bskul/_/zhugs sogs gong bzhin rab byas te/_/ye shes dbab pa sngon 'gro ba'i/_/mthong ba'i yul bzhin rnam par gnas/_/
[\[27b\]]
[\[27b.1\]]ye shes kyi ni 'khor lo la/_/gnyis med 'khor lo gzhug par bya/_/de nas byin gyis brlabs brjod la/_/sa bcu'i mgon po'i dkyil 'khor la/_/dngos grub rtags ni shes bya'i phyir/_/me tog 'dod par bya ba ste/_/sku'am dbus su nub gyur na/_/phyag rgya chen po bsgrub 
[\[27b.2\]]par bya/_/zhal gyi dkyil 'khor la babs na/_/sngags kyi dngos grub thob par bshad/_/lte ba'i dbus su rab babs na/_/thun mong dngos grub brgyad 'grub bo/_/sku smad cha ni gar 'bab kyang /_/zhi sogs dngos grub 'byung ba'o/_/ring zhing thal bas dngos grub kyang /_/da dung 
[\[27b.3\]]rgyang ring 'gyur ba'o/_/phyag rgya chen po ji lta bzhin/_/bsgrub pas ji bzhin dus thob bo/_/ye shes babs pa'i mthu nyid kyis/_/dman mchog gsum dang rang gi las/_/ji skad bshad bzhin gyis rab thob/_/de las gzhan pa ji snyed pa/_/bla ma'i ye shes stobs kyis ni/_/sbyar 
[\[27b.4\]]ba yis ni rnyed pa'o/_/de nas mkha' dbyings thams cad kun/_/sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang /_/rig pa'i lha mo khro bo tshogs/_/chu sogs rkyen bzung gang bar bsams/_/de dag kun gyis bum pa dang /_/gsang dang shes rab ye shes dang /_/brtul zhugs lung 
[\[27b.5\]]bstan dbugs dbyung dang /_/bka' stsal pa rnams rim bzhin dbang /_/de yis de yi byang chub ni/_/sku gsung thugs kyi byang chub che/_/sems can thams cad phan 'dod pas/_/ngo mtshar chen po rab tu grub/_/bsam gtan dkyil 'khor bsam gtan gyis/_/mchod dang 
[\[27b.6\]]dbang bskur de bzhin du/_/byas nas gzhan la 'ga' yi don/_/bsgrub phyir lung 'di bsgo ba'o/_/nga yis khyed ni dkyil 'khor du/_/bcug zin dbang bskur yang zin gyis/_/khyod kyis slob dpon sgrub po las/_/'dod bzhin du ni gyis shig ces/_/gnang nas bsnyen la 
[\[27b.7\]]sogs pa la/_/'jug par gang yin 'gyur ba ste/_/de yis kyang ni khyod shes bya/_/bsam gtan 'khor los sbyang phyir ro/_/gnyis pa de ru 'jug mi nus/_/mthong ba'i yul la mngon zhen zhing /_/blo gros ngan zhing sbyor ba spangs/_/dad ldan snod rung de mthong nas/_/don rnams 
[\[28a\]]
[\[28a.1\]]yal bar mi 'dor phyir/_/snying rje chen po rab bskyed nas/_/dag sogs cho ga sngon 'gro ba'i/_/phyi yi bya ba bsten bya ste/_/ye shes cung zad dbang gyur ltar/_/ri mo'i dkyil 'khor bris nas ni/_/dbang bskur yang dag brtsam bya ste/_/gnyis med man 
[\[28a.2\]]ngag gis sbyang bya/_/de bzhin sku sogs 'khor lo la/_/cho ga sngon bzhin rgyas bya ste/_kun khyab chos kyi dbyings ye shes/_/thams cad de ru rnam dag pas/_/bde gshegs gnas su mi bskyod dgod/_/gzhan gyi gnas su gzhan bkod de/_/gnyis shes rdo rje mtshon la sogs/_/
[\[28a.3\]]khyad par gzhan yang rtogs par bya/_/rdo rje slob dpon slob ma sogs/_/gong dang 'og gi cho ga rnams/_/sems kyi 'ba' zhig gtso bor gsungs/_/dman pa las ni mchog mi 'gyur/_/sems nyid glang po yan lta bu/_/gzugs la sogs la 'phyan byed pas/_/ngan 'gro g.yang 
[\[28a.4\]]sar ltung bar 'gyur/_/de nyid rab tu rtogs byas la/_/dran pa'i thag pa dam po yis/_/dmigs pa'i ka ba la btags te/_/'dod bzhin ba yi yul spyod pas/_/ji lta bzhin du 'dus par bya/_/ji skad du/_sems can kun gyi sems gang ni/_/brtan pa'i phyir na sems dpar 
[\[28a.5\]]brjod/_/mnyam par gzhag na ting 'dzin te/_/de bstan pa las rdo rjer 'gyur/_/sems dpa'i byin rlabs sbyor ba las/_/slar yang rdo rje sems dpar 'gyur/_/zhes so/_/bsod nams tshogs kyi yan lag phyir/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis lus ngag gi/_/bya ba bstan te drang srong ltar/_/
[\[28a.6\]]dang po rab tu 'drid pa'o/_/khang skas yan lag dang po bzhin/_/steng phyin ma yin dgos med min/_/de yi rten gyis de bzung ste/_/de nyid 'ba' zhig rtogs par bya/_/de bas sems la khyad par 'byung /_/longs spyod rdzogs dang sprul pa'i sku/_/de yi zhing khams yongs dag pa/_/sems nyid 
[\[28a.7\]]'ba' zhig las 'byung ste/_/mtshams kyi rnam shes ji lta dang /_/klu khros pa yi sems bzhin du/_/mi mthun ltar yang snang ba ste/_/de bas sems nyid rtogs par bya/_/rnal 'byor pa rnam pa gsum gyis slob ma rnams dbang bskur ba'i cho ga rgyas par bshad zin to//_//da ni dngos 
[\[28b\]]
[\[28b.1\]]grub sgrub pa'i thabs kyi cho ga bshad par bya'o/_/de la sgrub pa po'i gang zag ni/_/brtul zhugs sdom pa sngags ldan pa/_/mi brtan lha yi nga rgyal bral/_/cung zad ye shes dbang gyur pa/_/thun mong sgrub pa shes par bya/_/rgyu dang mi rgyur bcas pa rnams/_/sgyu 'drar sems can 
[\[28b.2\]]dbyer med rtogs/_/thun mong cig shos dngos grub kyi/_/sgrub par ye shes dbang gyur pa'o/_/de yi yid dang mthun pa'i gnas/_/shin tu dben pa'i ri dgon dang /_/rnam pa sna tshogs ri bo dang /_/klung dang 'bab chu rnams dang ni/_/dur gyi khrod la sogs par yang /_/bsam gtan 
[\[28b.3\]]bsdus pa 'di bsgom mo/_/dgon pa chen po'i sa phyogs ni/_/me tog 'bras bu sogs kyis brgyan/_/ri bo dben pa dag tu ni/_/dngos grub thams cad bsdus pas 'grub/_/lam gyi bzhi mdo shing gcig drung /_/mtshan ma gcig dang zhi gnas su/_/rdo rje dgug pa'i khyad par 
[\[28b.4\]]gyis/_/sgrub pa pos ni rtag tu bsgrub/_/dgon pa chen po'i sa phyogs sam/_/rab tu rnam par dben pa'am/_/ri bo nags 'dab chu ngogs su/_/ting 'dzin byas nas ting 'dzin 'thob/_/rdo rje 'gug gnas mig sman dang /_/ka pA la ru bsgom par bya/_/yid du 'ong dang shis pa'i gnas/_/
[\[28b.5\]]sgrub pa po yis rab nges bya/_/rjes su mthun pa'i shis pa'i zas/_/nyi ma dang ni zla ba dang /_/chu dang shel dang sman dang dri/_/phyag rgya chen po rig 'dzin gyis/_/lam la mkhas pa rnams kyis sngags/_/glang chen sha yis mngon shes bsgrub/_/rta yi sha yis mi snang bsgrub/_/'gugs pa 
[\[28b.6\]]ba lang sha yis bsgrub/_/khyi sha yis ni thams cad bsgrub/_/nus pa yis ni dgug par bya/_/med na bsam pas rnam par brtag/_/ngo bo nyid dang mthun dag dang /_/rang bzhin gyis ni de 'grub pa'o/_/de ltar byas na sangs rgyas dang /_/byang chub sems dpa' grags chen mnyes/_'dod 
[\[28b.7\]]khams 'jig rten dbang phyug gi/_/las kyi go 'phang thob par 'gyur/_/gtso bo gzi chen stobs dang ldan/_/mdangs bzang mthong na yid du 'ong /_/mthong bas bskul ba ma byas kyang /_/'jig rten 'di dag dbang du 'gyur/_/'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi/_/gsang ba dam pa byang chub ste/_/
[\[29a\]]
[\[29a.1\]]yang dag sngags kyi gsang ba 'di/_/rmongs pa'i man ngag sems 'da'o/_/mi snang stobs dang brtson 'grus dang /_/dam tshig chen po bsgrub pa dang /_/rdo rje 'gugs pa mchog dag kyang /_/dam tshig 'di yis rnam par grub/_/grub pa yi ni mtshan ma ni/_/shin tu 
[\[29a.2\]]'bebs pa thob pa yis/_/gdon mi za bar 'grub 'gyur te/_/bsod nams tshogs kyi yan lag rdzogs/_/spyi yi mtshan mar shes par bya/_/bzlas dang bsgom dang rang lha yi/_/khyad par gang yin rtogs par mdzad/_/rmi lam bye brag rab byed pas/_/so so'i mtshan ma shes par bya/_/
[\[29a.3\]]bsam gtan ngag gi bzlas pa yis/_/dus sam byin rlabs rab sdud du/_/'dod pas rnyed pa'i mtshan ma ni/_/rmi lam du ni shes par bya/_/rmi lam na sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs tha dad par mthong ba dang /_rmi lam na bdag nyid ye shes mchog thob nas sangs 
[\[29a.4\]]rgyas thams cad dang mthu mnyam par mthong ba dang /_rmi lam na mtshan dang dpe byad du ldan pa'i longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku rgyud dag pa la snang bar bdag nyid mthong ba dang /_rmi lam na brgya byin dang tshangs pa dang drag pa sogs pas bdag nyid la me tog la sogs pas mchod par mthong ba dang /_
[\[29a.5\]]rmi lam na sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis phyi nang gi 'dod pa'i yon tan lngas bdag la mchod par mthong ba dang /_rmi lam na bdag nyid rdo rje 'chang ba'am/_rtag pa la sogs pa'i gzugs su mthong ba dang /_rmi lam na rdo rje gsum mam spyan ma la sogs 
[\[29a.6\]]pa'am/_phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyis bdag la btud cing phyag 'tshal bar mthong ba dang /_rmi lam na mngon dga' la sogs pa'i zhing du mi bskyod pa la sogs pa mthong ba dang /_de dag kyang thugs rab tu dgyes nas chos kyi mdzod chen po gtad par mthong ba dang /_rmi 
[\[29a.7\]]lam na bdag nyid shAkya thub pa la sogs pa'i gzugs lha dang klu dang gnod sbyin la sogs pas bskor zhing chos bshad par mthong ba dang /_rmi lam na bdag nyid la sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis nam mkha' la gdugs dkar po bteg cing /_seng ge'i khri la 
[\[29b\]]
[\[29b.1\]]bzhag nas chos kyi rgyal por dbang bskur bar mthong ba dang /_rmi lam na gdol ba'i bu mo'am/_khyi'am/_rta rgod ma la sogs pa'am/_snyoms par 'jug pa de dag phyag rgya chen po grub pa'i mtshan ma'o/_/gzhan yang rmi lam na kha dog sna tshogs pa'i kun dga' ra ba dang skyed mos 
[\[29b.2\]]tshal yid du 'ong ba mthong ba dang /_rmi lam na lha'i bu mo gdu bu dang /_dpung rgyan dang /_snyan cha dang /_mgur chu dang /_nu 'bur dang /_zur phud la sogs pas brgyan pa dang /_mi'i khye'u dang bu mo yid du 'ong ba bcu drug pa rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa mthong ba ni thun mong gi 'grub pa'i 
[\[29b.3\]]mtshan ma'o/_/rigs 'dzin la sogs yongs smin las/_/'dzam bu gser 'dra'i sangs rgyas 'gyur/_/de bas de dag thams cad kun/_/cig shos mtshan mar shes par bya/_/sngon du bya ba'i las kyi rgyal po mchog ni/_sngags pa rnam par dag pa yis/_/de dag rab tu rtogs 
[\[29b.4\]]byas nas/_/sngon gyi phyir na gnyis kyis kyang /_/ci rigs gsang sngags bzlas brjod bya/_/dben par gtsang gtor byas nas su/_/bgegs bskrad sa la gsol ba btab/_/cig shos la yang gsol byas nas/_/bka' stsal sa ni brko bar bya/_/sngon gyi rim pa shes byas 
[\[29b.5\]]la/_/sa stengs rgyan dang ldan pa dang /_/dag bya'i phyir na gong bzhin du/_/dbang bskur blang bas yid sbyang bya/_/rdo rjes khyad par byas pa yis/_/sa 'dzin lta bur byin brlabs la/_/bdag nyid ting 'dzin gsum gnas pas/_/de dbus nga rgyal gsum ldan bya/_/sngon bzhin rtag sogs 
[\[29b.6\]]gsol btab nas/_/byin brlabs stangs thabs brtsam pa'o/_/phur bus btab nas sa srung ste/_/nub gcig sta gon gnas par bya/_/lha rnams gnas par byas nas su/_/brod bskyed mchod pa sna tshogs brtsam/_/dgongs par bya phyir gong ma bzhin/_/'khor lo gsol 
[\[29b.7\]]ba gdab par bya/_/lha mo bzhi dang mi bskyod pa/_/bdud rtsi 'khyil pa'i bum pa ni/_/rdzas dang ldan par sta gon bya/_/yod na bcu dgu nges pa'o/_/skud pa tshon rtsi 'dus byas la/_/sngon bzhin 'bebs pa'i cho ga bya/_/de yis thig dang tshon btab nas/_/zhal sogs ldan pa'i 
[\[30a\]]
[\[30a.1\]]phyag rgya dgod/_/gzhan la ma thos sems can rnams/_/gzung bar bya phyir sngon bzhin bshad/_/gnas rnams stong ba mi mdzes pas/_/dang chung srid pas de ma bsngags/_/mi rnams kyis ni phye ma yis/_/zhal la sogs pa rdzogs mi 'gyu/_/yan

[\[30a.3\]]bya phyir lha rnams la/_/ji srid rdzas kyi thab rnams su/_/sbyin sreg gis ni mnyes bya'o/_/slob ma gzhug pa'i rim pa bzhin/_/bdag nyid 'khor lor zhugs par bya/_/sdig bshags me tog 'dor ba'i bar/_/byas nas lha rnams kyis gnang ba'i/_/sngon bzhin bum pa gsang ba 
[\[30a.4\]]dang /_/shes rab ye shes brtul zhugs dang /_/lung bstan dbugs dbyung bka' stsal thob/_/de yis bsgrub pa brtsam pa'o/_/sgrub pa'i thabs gang zhe na/_dang po de bzhin gshegs pa yi/_/rig 'dzin sgrub thabs bshad par bya'o/_/ji bzhin byas pa'i sngags pa yis/_/thun

[\[30a.7\]]swa b+hA wa At+ma ko\u0f85haM/_gnyis sogs ma gtogs de phyag rgyas/_/gnas nas sngags 'di bsgom par bya/_/oM AHoM dzi na dzi ga hU~M/_brjod pas oM las 'od zer tshogs/_/dkar ba'i rgyun gyis nang gsal te/_/ba spu'i bu ga nas spros pa'i/_/'od rtse rtag pa'i tshogs rnams 
[\[30b\]]
[\[30b.1\]]kyis/_/nam mkha'i khams kun bkang byas nas/_/sangs rgyas rtag pa spyan drangs nas/_/rang gi ba spu'i bu gar gzud/_/brtan pa'i ngo bos phyag byas nas/_/yang nas yang du dus bzhir bsgom/_/de nas zhag bdun la sogs nas/_/nye ba'i mtshan ma mthong nas ni/_/'byung

[\[30b.3\]]ba'i 'od/_/rdo rje bskal pa gsum du gnas/_/thams cad sngon bzhin rab byas te/_/'od dpag med bsgoms mgrin pa ru/_/AHlas pad+ma AHyis mtshan/_/bsams nas bzlas brjod yang bya'o/_/oM AHA ro l-ika hU~M/_/de yis sngon bzhin 'od dpag med/_/spros te bkug

[\[31a\]]
[\[31a.1\]]thams cad du/_/mi bskyod rdo rje mchod par 'gyur/_/rin chen 'byung ldan sngon bzhin bskyed/_/thugs kar mtshan ma la gnas pa'i/_/oM bzlas pa yis 'grub pa'o/_/de dang 'dra bar bsgrub par bya/_/oM AHoM sa man+ta dz+nyA na rastu hU~M/_de yis rin chen 'byung

[\[31a.3\]]med pa bsgom byas la/_/de yi thugs kar pad+ma la/_/gnas pa'i oM ni bzlas byas pas/_/dpag med 'dra ba bsgrub par bya/_/oM AHoM pra t+ya bek+Sha Na dz+nyA na rastu hU~M/_'od dpag med pa'i tshogs spros pas/_/bkug nas brtan byas chags byas nas/_/gong ma bzhin du rab bsgrubs nas/_/

[\[31a.5\]]sngags kyi bzlas brjod brtsam pa'o/_/oM AHoM kri ta a nuSh+Tha na dz+nyA na rastu hU~M/_de yis don yod grub pa'i tshogs/_/spros te bkug byas zhugs byas la/_/gnas brtan ngo bor chags byas na/_/zla ba phyed na de dag 'grub/_/sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje yi/_/rdo rje thogs med 'dra ba 'ong /_/de ni

[\[31a.7\]]byas te/_/snang mdzad mgo mgrin thugs ka ru/_/'khor lo pad+ma rdo rje la/_/zla ba'i dbus nas oM gsum gnas/_/oM AHoM oM oM tri ga ka ra ya rastu hU~M/_de yis rdo rje gsum gyi tshogs/_/bkug nas gnas gsum du brtan par/_/byas nas nye ba'i mtshan ma mthong /_/gong bzhin 
[\[31b\]]
[\[31b.1\]]byas na zla phyed 'grub/_/sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje yi/_/sangs rgyas snang mdzad 'dra ba'i mdog_/de ni byang chub ye shes dang /_/sku gsum ye shes 'grub par 'gyur/_/de yi rim pas mi bskyod sogs/_/rang rang ngo bos rab bsgrubs na/_/dpal ldan zla ba phyed grub nas/_/

[\[31b.5\]]ba phyed nas 'grub/_/ri rab sum cu rtsa drug gi/_/rab tu phra ba'i rdul snyed kyi/_/rdo rje 'dzin dang 'dra ba kun/_/de yi rjes su 'brang bar 'gyur/_/sngon bzhin mi bskyod rab bsgoms la/_/rdo rje dngos byas hU~M bskyed de/_/rdo rje rigs kyi 'khor lo bsgom/_/sngags kyi bzlas brjod bya

[\[31b.7\]]yon tan can gyi bdag gir 'gyur/_/drag po dag gis phyag byas pas/_/khams gsum rdo rje chen por 'gyur/_/sngon bzhin rgyas par rin chen 'byung /_/bsgoms nas swa bskyed rin chen dngos/_/rin chen rigs kyi lha bsgoms la/_/sngags kyi bzlas brjod bya ba'o/_/oM 
[\[32a\]]
[\[32a.1\]]AHrat+na d+h+r-ika rat+na ar+tha sid+d+hi swA hA/_rin chen rigs kyi lha rnams kun/_/spro zhing bsdu bas brtan byas nas/_/nye ba'i mtshan mthong sngon bzhin bsgoms/_/zla ba phyed 'grub the tshom med/_/ye shes lnga ldan bsten na ni/_/bskal ba gsum gyi yun gnas shing /_/
[\[32a.2\]]phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyis/_/gsang ba gsum la bsnyen bkur byed/_/de bzhin dpag med tshe bsgoms la/_/gser sogs pad+ma AHlas skyes/_/lte bar gsung rigs lha bsgoms la/_/bzlas brjod rab tu bya ba'o/_/o AHA ro l-ika ka ma la ar+tha sid+d+hi 
[\[32a.3\]]swA hA/_de spros gsung gi rigs bkug nas/_/bcug nas brtan bya yang yang bsgom/_/mtshan ma thob nas gtor ma btang /_/zla ba phyed na de 'grub 'gyur/_/ri rab sum cu rtsa drug gi/_/rab tu phra ba'i rdul snyed la/_/sangs rgyas rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor la/_/dag pa'i bdag nyid 
[\[32a.4\]]'jog par byed/_/don yod grub pa sngon bzhin bsgom/_/ral gri dngos byas ha las bskyed/_/las kyi lha rnams yu bar bsgom/_/sngags kyi bzlas pa brtsam pa'o/_/oM AHpradz+nyA d+h+r-ika khad+ga ar+tha sid+d+hi swA hA/_las kyi rigs kyi lha rnams spro/_/bsdu zhing bcug nas chags 
[\[32a.5\]]byas na/_/de mtshan mthong nas sngon bzhin bsgrub/_/zla phyed kyis ni nges par 'grub/_/khams gsum kun gyis rab mchod cing /_/tshangs la sogs pas phyag kyang byas/_/stong gsum na ni mchog tu dpa'/_/mi mchog gsang ba 'dzin par 'gyur/_/gang rnams 'dod

[\[32b\]]
[\[32b.1\]]oM AHhaM many+dzu shrI hU~M phaT/_de yi 'od kyis nang gsal nas/_/ba spu las byung 'od zer lngas/_/thams cad 'jam dpal skur byas te/_/bkug cing bu gar thim par bya/_/yang nas yang du brtan byas pas/_/ba spu dbang po gzhu mthong nas/_/'byung po gtor byin chags byas 
[\[32b.2\]]na/_/zla ba phyed kyis nges par 'grub/_/'jam dpal dam tshig longs spyod la/_/sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje can/_/sa bcu dag la gnas pa yi/_/byang chub sems dpa' de 'gyur ro/_/yang ni 'jam dpal de bzhin bsgom/_/ral gri yu bar bcug 'phan gnas/_/bzlas 
[\[32b.3\]]brjod byas la gong bzhin dpag_/de dang 'dra bar bsgrub par bya/_/oM AHth+liM many+dzu hU~M swA hA/_de ni zla ba phyed na yang /_/'od zer 'bar bar gyur pa yin/_/rdul phran rdo rje lus ngag gi/_/rdo rje sku dang 'dra bar 'gyur/_/snga ma bzhin du kun shes bya/_/gshin rje gshed la sogs pa bcu/_/
[\[32b.4\]]longs spyod dang po rdzogs pa la/_/mtshan ma yis ni de 'dra 'grub/_/tho ba dbyug pa pad+ma dang /_/rdo rje lcags kyu dbyug gsal zhing /_/ral gri 'khor lo rdo rje rnams/_/dngos byas 'khor lo gcig bsgom brjod/_/oM AHya mAn+ta kr-ita mud+ha ra sid+d+hi hU~M/_oM AHpradz+nyAn+ta kr-ita badzra d+ha na da sid+d+hi 
[\[32b.5\]]hU~M/_oM AHpad+mAn+ta kr-ita pad+ma sid+d+hi hU~M/_oM AHbig+h+nAn+ta kr-ita badzra sid+d+hi hU~M/_oM AHTAk+ki rA dza aM ku sha sid+d+hi hU~M/_oM AHnI la daN+DI daN+Da sid+d+hi hU~M/_oM AHma hA ba la tri shU la sid+d+hi hU~M/_oM AHa tsa la khat+ga sid+d+hi hU~M/_oM AHtsakra warti tsakra sid+d+hi hU~M/_oM AHsuM b+ha badzro sid+d+hi hU~M/_de yis khro bo'i 
[\[32b.6\]]tshogs bkug ste/_/bcug nas brtan par byas nas su/_/sngon bzhin gtor sogs kyis bsgrub na/_de 'dra zla ba phyed na 'grub/_/yang na rtag pa 'od dpag med/_/mi bskyod pa yi mtshan ma rnams/_/bsgrubs te rang gi lha bcug pas/_/zla phyed de dang 'dra bar 'gyur/_/mi snang ba 
[\[32b.7\]]bsgrub pa'i cho ga bshad par bya'o/_/sngon du spyi yi cho ga sogs/_/rgyas par rab tu bshad 'gyur yang /_/bde blag tu ni rtogs bya'i phyir/_/yang ni thugs rjes bsdus te bstan/_/mtshan ma thob pa'i rnal 'byor pas/_/'dzab bzlas cho ga la sogs nas/_/lhas dbang bskur ba'i 
[\[33a\]]
[\[33a.1\]]par zhugs la/_/bde ba'i stan la 'dug par bya/_/bgegs bzlog cho ga nas brtsams te/_/brgyad kyis nam mkha' snying po bskyed/_/chos dbyings byin rlabs lus kun la/_/gnas bzhi ma gtogs khaM gis mtshan/_/oM AHkhaM ga na kha gar+b+ha hU~M/
[\[33a.2\]]de yi 'od kyis sangs rgyas rnams/_/nam mkha'i snying po'i skur byas la/_/bkug ste khaM kun la bstims la/_/nam mkha' bzhin du stong par bsam/_/bdag gi yan lag mi mthong sogs/_/'byung po gtor ma yang byin te/_/chags byas [(]yad [.]yang [)]du bsgrub 'gyur na/_/zla ba 
[\[33a.3\]]phyed na mi snang 'gyur/_/sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' yis/_/yang dang yang du mchod pa dang /_/bskal pa gcig tu gnas pa dang /_/sangs rgyas kyis kyang mi gzigs 'gyur/_/gzhi yi 'bras bu gzhan yin phyir/_/de bshad gsang ba'i thig le las/_/rim pa 'di 
[\[33a.4\]]bzhin tshig 'grub pa/_/'jig rten dbang po bsgrub pa bshad/_/thams cad sngon bzhin rab byas la/_/spyan ras gzigs dbang bsgom par bya/_/de lce hrIHlas pad+ma ni/_/'dab brgyad mdun du rdo rje ni/_/oM AHhrIHlo ke shwa ra b+ha d+ha na ar+tha sid+d+hi hU~M swA hA/_bzlas pa thos pas sangs 
[\[33a.5\]]rgyas rnams/_/'jig rten dbang ni bskur byas te/_/bkug ste lce la bcug nas ni/_/gnas brtan ngo bor chags par bya/_/lce de rdo rje mthong gyur nas/_/gtor ma byin te yang yang bsgom/_/zla ba phyed na nges pa ru/_/tshig gi dngos grub thams cad 'grub/_/bden pa ru ni smra ba

[\[46b.1\]]yis mtshan/_/de yis sangs rgyas thams cad kun/_/mkha' 'gro'i gzugs byas hri la zhugs/_/de gnyis rdo rje mkha' 'gror bskyed/_/drag po mi sdug khros pa ni/_/ut+pal sngon po lta bu la/_/g.yas dkar g.yon dmar phyag drug pa/_/dang po rdo rje mkha' 'gro

[\[49a.1\]]pa'i/_/kha~M ni brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas na/_/dur khrod sa la sbrul gzugs bri/_/nag po mi sdug 'jigs byed pa/_/'od 'bar sems 'khrugs mche phreng gtams/_/lce gnyis myug pa mig bgrad pa/_/mjug ma rdeb pa'i tshul can no/_/de yi kha ru mi bzad

[\[49b\]]
[\[49b.1\]]thun bzhir zhag bdun du/_/bsnun zhing brdungs pas bsgrub bya de/_/g.yang du ltung nas 'chi bar nges/_/yang na de yi rnal 'byor gyis/_/g.yas par hU~M las byung ba yi/_/rdo rje rtse lngas bsgrub bya yi/_/lus la bsnun zhing 'di brjod do/_/oM hrIHSh+T+rIHwi kr-i tA na na che ge mo badzra a ko Ta ya

[\[50a\]]
[\[50a.1\]]gsungs sngags dang bskul bar bya/_/tsher ma'i me la khros te bsreg_/thun bzhir zhag bdun brgya rtsa brgyad/_/bzlas pas sangs rgyas kyis sbyar ba'i/_/las kyang 'jig na dgra bo yi/_/sbyar ba'i las ni smos ci dgos/_/bsad pas de dag thams cad 
[\[50a.2\]]kun/_/thugs rjes bzung phyir rnal 'byor ches/_/gnyis bral 'das pa'i de ma thag_/'jug pa'i las 'di brtsam pa'o/_/de dag rnams kyis dngos po rnams/_/ma skyes par ni gzhig nas kyang /_/'bras bu myong bar nges pa'i tshul/_/ye shes rang rig chen po mchog_/
[\[50a.3\]]sa bon 'od zer gyis bkug nas/_/zhal du zhugs nas zhu gyur te/_/rdo rje lam byung pad+mar gnas/_/ye shes bdud rtsi'i gzugs gyur la/_/spyan sogs lha mos bskul pa la/_/hU~M las rdo rje hU~M gis mtshan/_/spro bsdus mi bskyod rdo rjer bskyed/_/chos kyi dbyings su byin gyis 
[\[50a.4\]]brlab/_/rang las dgu gnyis drug phyung nas/_/gnyis dang gsum du rdzogs byas la/_/dang po rtog pa yongs dag phyir/_/sngags kyis phur bu dbyung bar bya/_/oM AHbadzra kI li ki lA ya hU~M/_phyung nas de yi gnas su yang /_/rang gi ye shes 'khor lo can/_/gnas bcas 
[\[50a.5\]]bsams nas 'od zer gyis/_/sngon bzhin rang sgra sangs rgyas su/_/bskyed dang des bskyed lha mo'i tshogs/_/bdud rtsi lngas gang bum pa yis/_/dbang bskur de dang bla mar bcas/_/zhugs pas rdo rje sems dpar bskyed/_/oM badzra d+h+r-ika sngags kyis phyung ste/_/mdun du rlung gi 
[\[50a.6\]]dkyil 'khor steng /_/'khor lo bcas pa mos byas la/_/mchod bstod bdud rtsi myang ba bya/_/a ho yaM gi sngags brjod pas/_/rlung gis bteg nas mngon dga' ru/_/gshegs su gsol ba'i sngags 'phangs pas/_/de dag sras su skye bar nges/_/rang rang lha yi sbyor 
[\[50a.7\]]ba'am/_/de yi gnas pa gang rung du/_/blo gros ldan pa'i rnal 'byor pas/_/sbyor ba de kho na yis bya/_/gnyis po rang zhugs de ma thag_/de yi ye shes rang lha'am/_/zhe sdang dag pa'i sbyor kho nas/_/gong ma bzhin du rab tu brtsam/_/bdag med sems med bems po ni/_/
[\[50b\]]
[\[50b.1\]]lan gsum gyis ni gzhig byas pas/_/gong gi sbyor bas 'bras bu che/_/mjug tu brtsam mi dgos par 'byung /_/de ltar thabs dang sngags ldan pas/_/'khor ba'i 'og ma'i rgyu ldan pa/_/bsad pas rang dang gzhan dag pas/_/ma 'khrul pa ru gsungs pa'o/_/sred pa

[\[63b.1\]]dang yang du nyin mtshan du/_/zla ba drug tu bsgrub par bya/_/de yis sgrub po bdag nyid kyis/_/phyi nang dus su sangs rgyas kyi/_/mtshan gyis brgyan par ma gyur na/_/yang ni cho ga de kho nas/_/de yi 'dod pa longs spyod cing /_/gsang ba'i mchod pa dang bcas pas/_/sa bcu de la 
[\[63b.2\]]gnas bcas te/_/zla ba drug tu yang bsgrub bya/_/gal te de ru sgrub pa pos/_/gong gsungs mthong bar ma gyur na/_/ji skad bshad pa'i cho ga yis/_/yang ni zla drug rab tu brtsams/_/drug gsum bco brgyad cho ga bzhin/_/bsgrub pas rdo rje gsum gyis ni/_/dngos grub 
[\[63b.3\]]ster te phyis 'byung ba'i/_/phyag rgya chen po rab tu 'thob/_/de ltar byas pa'i sgrub pa pos/_/bsgoms pas dngos grub mchog ma mthong /_/snyan tshig zhi ba'i bskul ba yis/_/dngos grub bslang bar bya ba'o/_/sngon gyi 'khor lo khang pa'am/_/gzhan yang gang yang rung ba 
[\[63b.4\]]ru/_/bgegs bskrad phur bus btab nas su/_/sngon bzhin gtsang gtor sa gsol btab/_/sangs rgyas la sogs snga ma bzhin/_/gsol btab stegs bu 'dus byas te/_/byin brlabs ting 'dzin gsum gnas la/_/skye gcig thogs bsam gsol ba gdab/_/
[\[63b.5\]]drug cu rtsa bzhis bgegs bskrad nas/_/sa bsrungs sa yi lha mo yang /_/mchod pa sogs kyis mnyes byas la/_/dbang por zhal gyis bzhes par bya/_/de nas me tog sna tshogs dang /_/dri zhim la sogs ldan pa yi/_/maN+Dal sngon bzhin 'dus byas la/_/de 
[\[63b.6\]]na gnas pa dmigs par bya/_/rang gi sa bon 'od zer las/_/lcags kyu rdo rje gsal shing sogs/_/mtshan pa'i khro bo spro byas la/_/de dag mchod sogs kyis mnyes bya/_/sgrub pos 'og nas 'byung ba yi/_/tshigs bcad sngags kyi khyed rnams kyis/_/sangs rgyas 
[\[63b.7\]]byang chub sems dpa' rnams/_/bskul cig ces ni smra bar bya/_/de nas zhal gyis bzhes nas ni/_/song ste 'jig rten khams gnas pa'i/_/sangs rgyas sogs la phra mo dang /_/snyan tshig 'jebs pa'i dbyangs kyis bskul/_/che ge zhes bya rdo rje che/_/'di yi gnas 
[\[64a\]]
[\[64a.1\]]su dam sdom can/_/rig ma'i brtul zhugs spyod pa yis/_/tshogs ni rdzogs par 'gyur ba'o/_/bsgom pa bzlas brjod yongs rdzogs nas/_/phyag rgya chen po bsgrub pa'i phyir/_/brtul zhugs spyod pa yang gnas nas/_/khyed kyis bsgrub 
[\[64a.2\]]pa'i dkyil 'khor bri/_/khyed cag de ru byon nas su/_/de yi 'khor lo byin brlabs la/_/mchod pa la sogs bzhes nas kyang /_/phyag rgya chen po gnang bar mdzod/_/de ltar brjod pas de dag kyang /_/rab tu dgyes nas khro bo la/_/de ltar bya zhes bsams nas kyang /_/lhan 
[\[64a.3\]]cig byon bsams gnas su dgod/_/khro bos sgrub pa po la smras/_/bdag cag rnams kyis gsol ba btab/_/de dag kyang ni 'di skad gsungs/_/rdo rje che ge zhes bya la/_/bdag cag phyag rgya chen po bzhin/_/gsungs nas 'di ru byon pa lags/_/khyed kyis mchod pa'i 
[\[64a.4\]]tshogs rnams kyis/_/mnyes par byas te dam tshig bskul/_/de nas sgrub pos sangs rgyas dang /_/khro rgyal lha mo mnyes bya'i phyir/_/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams bkug nas/_/rang gi ba spu'i bu gar bcug_/de nyid lha mo sgeg pas brgyan/_/spros nas sangs rgyas byang chub 
[\[64a.5\]]sems/_/khro bo dang bcas 'khor lo la/_/bde ba chen po'i ros mchod pa/_/byas nas bstod la bdud rtsi myang /_/sna tshogs kyis ni mnyes byas nas/_/srog dang bcas pa bsams nas kyang /_/bdag nyid kyis ni snyan gson gsol/_/sangs rgyas kun bdag dngos 
[\[64a.6\]]grub kyi/_/dkyil 'khor bdag gis bri bar 'tshal/_/sang ni de ru byon nas kyang /_/bdag gi mchod pa la sogs bzhes/_/bzhes nas bdag la dngos grub stsol/_/gal te ma stsal khyed rnams kun/_/rang nyid kyi ni bstan pa la/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]du smra bar gyur pa'o/_/de

[\[64b\]]
[\[64b.1\]]mchod nas ji bzhin gnas su dgod/_/de nas phyi de nyin par yang /_/skud pa tshon rtsi sngon bzhin bsgrub/_/gos dang rin chen rgyan ldan pa'i/_/khye'u dang bu mo dbab byas nas/_/de yis dngos grub dkyil 'khor mchog_/yang dag sbyin byed bris nas kyang /_/dbye ba byas

[\[64b.4\]]gsungs mchod pa nyi shus kyang /_/mchod nas 'od zer rtse mo las/_/yid dga'i rin chen sna bdun spro/_/gser mdog lta bu'i rgyan rnams kyis/_/brgyan pa'i bde ba'i longs spyod 'dzin/_/pad+ma'i steng na gnas pa yi/_/bud med rin chen dbul bar bya/_/gnod sbyin lta 
[\[64b.5\]]bu'i gzugs 'dzin pa/_/gos sngon bsgos pa rin chen gter/_/gzung ba'i khyim bdag rin po che/_/sgyu ma lta bus sprul pa bya/_/mche ba drug ldan glang po dkar/_/gser gyis brgyan pa spro bar bya/_/ut+pal mthing ga'i 'dab 'dra'i rta/_/rin chen chas brgyan spros te

[\[66a\]]

[\[66b.1\]]bya/_/sngon gsungs 'phros pa rnams kyis kyang /_/rang khams gnas pa'i rtag pa sogs/_/rang rang kha dog dang ldan pa/_/lcags kyus bkug par byas nas su/_/phyag rgya'i gsang bar nyi ma'i steng /_/me yi dkyil 'khor gru gsum du/_/bzhag ste de dag dam tshig gis/_/

[\[66b.3\]]gcig gzugs/_/rdo rje las ni dbyung bar bya/_/de yis de dag rtag pa yi/_/spyi bo nas ni sngags 'di yis/_/btab pas rlung gis thal ba ltar/_/sngags kyi lus ni mi snang byas/_/oM ts+tshin+d+ha ts+tshin+d+ha/_ha na ha na/_ha na ha na/_dIp+ta badzra tsakra hU~M phaT/_de bzhin pad+ma'i rigs 'byung ba'i/_/

[\[67a\]]
[\[67a.1\]]ya hU~M/_oM AHsarba kI la ya ka na saM g+ha sa te na badzra di ta a badzra ya hU~M/_ye shes mar me lta bu'i tshul/_/rang gi mi shes dbyer med byas/_dbugs 'byin mchog dang lhan cig tu/_/cho ga 'di ni ma lus brtsam/_/dpung [{]brkyang ma[,]rkyang ma[}]gul pa nas 'khyud

[\[67b\]]
[\[67b.1\]]snang bar byed/_/de nyid yang dag ye shes thob/_/gzugs brnyan bdag nyid spyi gzugs kyis/_/'khor lo gsum po mtha' yas khams/_/'gro ba'i phan don mdzad pa'o/_/'khor ba ji srid gnas kyi bar/_/sbyor ba bdun ldan rdo rje sems/_/ro myang mtshan nyid brgyan

[\[68a.1\]]de/_/sgyu ma lta bur de bzhin no/_/dper na yid bzhin rin po che/_/rtog pa thams cad rab bral yang /_/skal ba ldan pa'i sems dbang gis/_/'dod pa thams cad 'byung bar 'gyur/_/de bzhin byang chub sems de las/_/chos rnams thams cad 
[\[68a.2\]]'byung bas na/_/nam mkha' rjes 'brang chos rnams la/_/sangs rgyas chos rnams rjes su 'brang /_/de bas dngos grub thams cad dang /_/sangs rgyas chos rnams thams cad kyi/_/lus dang ngag dang sems la gnas/_/de ni khams gsum mi gnas so/_/nam mkha' 
[\[68a.3\]]bzhin du dag pa'i phyir/_/de bzhin gcig la gcig mi gnas/_/de dag de bzhin nyid gnas pas/_/de ni smra brjod yul las 'das/_/de 'dra'i de bzhin nyid la ni/_/rang gi ye shes rab sbyor zhing /_/gzhan yang bdag po 'di snyam du/_/sku mi 'gyur ba 'di snyam du/_/
[\[68a.4\]]gsung dang thugs kyi bdag nyid med/_/nam mkha' rdo rje rtog las byung /_/log pa dag tu 'dzin snyam nas/_/bdag gcig ston pa mi gsung bzhugs/_/de nas 'jam pa'i dbyangs la sogs/_/de yi gnyis pa dgag pa'i phyir/_/bde bar gshegs la yang zhus pa/_/gsung 
[\[68a.5\]]la log 'byung mi brtag gsol/_/ci phyir sngon gyi rgyal ba ltar/_/rdo rje 'chang bas mdzad par rigs/_/yod de tshangs pa la sogs pa/_/yang dag mngon shes ma thob pas/_/kun rdzob rgyun la 'khrul pas na/_/bdag po 'di yis shes nas sam/_/yang na mi sems 'chad do 
[\[68a.6\]]sems/_/de bas log 'byung bkag pa'o/_/skye med rdo rje mngon byang chub/_/ngo bo med pa mi 'gyur ba/_/bde gshegs rnams kyis kyang mi shes/_/des ni sems can kun la khyab/_/des na de dag thams cad kun/_/byang chub rdo rje'i gnas thob pas/_/sangs rgyas rdo rje rdzu 
[\[68a.7\]]'phrul che/_/de blangs de ni thob pa'o/_/de bas chos 'di thams cad kun/_/ngo bo nyid bdag gnas med la/_/chos dbyings shes rab gnas su rtog_/rdo rje las byung ltar smras pa/_/smra ste smra ba yang ma yin/_/sngon bzhin thams cad mkha' la gnas/_/nam mkha' gang du 
[\[68b\]]
[\[68b.1\]]mi gnas pas/_/de ni rang bzhin 'od gsal dbyings/_/de ru yang dag kun rdzob rgyan/_/dbyer med pa yis rnam par bsgom/_/de ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi/_/byang chub sems ni bsgom pa'o/_/de ltar byang chub sems bsgom pas/_/rnam dag gsang ba'i yi ge 
[\[68b.2\]]gsum/_/chos dang longs spyod sprul pa'i sku/_/dad pa'i 'bras bu rdzogs par 'gyur/_/gang phyir bud med skyes pa dang /_/ma ning gsal byed thams cad ni/_/yi ge gsum sdud de yi phyir/_/de ni kun gyi gtso bo'o/_/dngos po gzugs can thams cad dang /_/sgra 
[\[68b.3\]]grag pa ni ji snyed dang /_/bsam rtog ma lus thams cad kun/_/rdo rje gsum gyi sdud pa'o/_/de 'dra'i de nyid gsum pos ni/_/kun khyab gzhan gyi spyod yul min/_/nyag gcig rtogs pa'i spyod yul pas/_/de ni gsang ba'i yi ge'o/_/de bas rdzogs ldan la 
[\[68b.4\]]sogs par/_/de ni snod ni ma gzigs pas/_/rdo rje 'chang ba de yi tshe/_/de 'gyur gsang ba'i don ma bshad/_/pha rol phyin pa la sogs pas/_/bskal par 'bad kyang mi brnyes pa/_/gsang ba'i don 'dis tshe 'di la/_/skad cig thang cig yud tsam gyis/_/sangs 
[\[68b.5\]]rgyas kun gyi byang chub che/_/sku gsum dbyer med sangs rgyas mchog_/rdo rje 'chang ba kun gyi gnas/_/bla na med pa des 'gyur ro/_/gong gsungs sems can de dag kun/_/'di yi snod ma yin thos nas/_/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che/_/snying rje gar gyis 
[\[68b.6\]]de dag dus/_/don de zab mor bstan pa'i phyir/_/gsang ba'i yi ge rnam dag pas/_/thun mong gi ni sdug bsngal te/_/de bas bde gshegs rnams kyis bkag_/dus 'dir bdag cag snod du gyur/_/de yi chos nyid nyer blangs pas/_/sku gsum rdo rje'i chos nyid 
[\[68b.7\]]de/_/'ga' 'gas rtogs par 'gyur ba'o/_/de ltar bstan pa'i dngos po rnams/_/rnam pa bzhi ru yang dag dbye/_/skyes bu lam dang de sngon 'gro/_/lam gyis bsgrub pa'i 'bras bu'o/_/skyes bu gsum de dang po dang /_/cung zad tsam dang ye shes thob/_/bsnyen pa thun 
[\[69a\]]
[\[69a.1\]]mong cig shos kyi/_/bsgrub la 'jug pa'i skyes bu'o/_/rdo rje can gyis bstan pa yi/_/lam gyi rim pa rnam gnyis te/_/bskyed pa yi ni rim pa dang /_/rdzogs pa'i rim pa kho na'o/_/bskyed pa'i rim pa bsnyen pa dang /_/thun mong thun min sgrub 
[\[69a.2\]]pa la/_/snod dag skye ba'i rim pa gsum/_/bsgom pa'i rim pa ji snyed pa'o/_/rdzogs pa'i rim pa rnal 'byor dang /_/phyag rgya las skyes chos thams cad/_/sgyu ma la sogs 'drar bsgrub pa/_/rang bzhin gnas pa'i rnal 'byor ro/_/de yi sngon 'gro rnam gsum ste/_/
[\[69a.3\]]dkyil 'khor dang ni dam tshig dang /_/de yi bsgrub pa'i ngo bo nyid/_/rdo rje 'chang bas rab tu gsungs/_/dkyil 'khor bzhi ste ri mo dang /_/lus dang byang sems b+ha ga ste/_/ye shes lnga la sogs pa yi/_/thob byed gnas su yang dag bshad/_/dam tshig la ni rnam gnyis 
[\[69a.4\]]te/_/thun mong sdom par bstan pa dang /_/thun mong ma yin sdom pa'o/_/thun mong ma yin rnam gsum ste/_/spang ba'i dam tshig sdom pa dang /_/bza' dang bsrung ba'i dam tshig dang /_/rnal 'byor snying po sdom pa ste/_/snod sogs rkyen du yang dag bshad/_/dbang gi rnam 
[\[69a.5\]]pa gsum dag ste/_/bum pa'i dbang gi dang po dang /_/gnyis pa la ni gsang ba'i dbang /_/shes rab ye shes gsum pa'o/_/de ltar de bzhin bzhi pa yang /_/bla ma dam pa'i zhal las ni/_/thob pa yin te rnam pa gsum/_/kun la rab tu khyab pa'o/_/'bras bu 
[\[69a.6\]]la ni rnam gnyis te/_/thun mong gi ni grub pa dang /_/phyag rgya chen po'i grub pa'o/_/thun mong grub pa rnam pa brgyad/_/rig 'dzin mngon par shes pa lnga/_/mngag gzhug sngags kyi khyad par dang /_/a dza las kyi dbye ba'o/_/cig shos rdo rje gsum yin te/_/de dang 
[\[69a.7\]]de ru rab bstan pa'i/_/sangs rgyas nyid grub dngos grub ni/_/yang dag ye shes dbang thob pa'o/_/rnal 'byor chen po'i rgyud rnams kun/_/tshul 'di las ni ma 'das pas/_/shes rab ldan pa rnams kyis kyang /_/'di rnams kho na bsgre bar bya/_/gsang dang rab gsang 
[\[69b\]]
[\[69b.1\]]shin tu gsang /_/gsang ba 'dus pa zhes bya'i rgyud/_/phra zhing shes dka' bde gshegs rnams kyi yul 'di rtog bral dpyad las 'das/_/'on kyang sangs rgyas ye shes zhabs kyi bla med thugs rje nyi zer gyis/_/bskal par 'dus byas pa yi mun pa kun bsal bde gshegs 
[\[69b.2\]]spyod yul bstan/_/bdag gis cung zad rtogs nas dang po grogs mchog bzang po gsang ba dang /_/ko sa la yi grogs mchog dri med pa yi bzang po zhes bya dang /_/shes rab lha zhes bya ba'i grogs mchog pany+tsa la yi dur khrod gnas/_/legs pa'i tshul mang shri na len+d+ra yi 
[\[69b.3\]]gtsug lag khang gnas te/_/dge ba'i spun gyis gsol btab rgyud kyi rgyal po de la blo dman pas/_/tshogs ni chung ngus rtogs par bya phyir nga rgyal ma yin dad pa yis/_/bsdus pa'i don dang sgrub pa'i thabs 'di 'bad pas sems khral cher byas la/_/sangs rgyas la 
[\[69b.4\]]sogs dpa' bo mkhas pa rnams kyis bdag la bzod par mdzod/_/de ltar byas pa las byung dge ba dri med de yis 'gro ba rnams/_/'di gzhung rim pas tshogs ni rab rdzogs rig 'dzin la sogs gsum gyis ni/_/tha mal pa yi skad cig sogs bsgres de 'gag de yis 
[\[69b.5\]]bzlog bya ste/_/rang gi phyag rgyas rdo rje 'chang ba thob nas bdud las rgyal gyur cig_/dpal gsang ba 'dus pa sgrub pa'i thabs dngos grub 'byung ba'i gter zhes bya ba/_slob dpon chen po bi ta pA das mdzad pa rdzogs so//_//rgya gar gyi mkhan po ka ma la gu h+ya dang /_
[\[69b.6\]]bod kyi lo tsA ba lha btsan po mnga' bdag lha ye shes rgyal mtshan gyis bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa'o//[
